- **Coordinator bypass verdict tracking** (Issue #1279): Extended `record_plan_critic_skipped()` with optional `bypass_reason` parameter and added new `write_coordinator_bypass_verdict()` function in `pipeline_completion_state.py` to enable drain-queue coordinators to record machine-readable audit trails when bypassing plan-critic for mechanical extensions. New `/drain-queue` command documentation section "Coordinator Bypass of plan-critic" details the two-call protocol. 3 new tests in `tests/regression/test_issue_1279_coordinator_bypass_verdict.py` verify the bypass tracking logic.
- **Spec test deletion scope guard in unified_pre_tool.py** (Issue #790): `unified_pre_tool.py` now blocks deletion or truncation of `tests/spec_validation/test_spec_issue{N}_*.py` files that belong to a different issue than the current pipeline run. New `_check_spec_test_deletion_scope(file_path)` function extracts the issue number from the spec test filename pattern and compares it against `_get_current_issue_number()`; a mismatch blocks the operation with a REQUIRED NEXT ACTION directing the agent to archive the file to `tests/archived/` instead. New `_extract_bash_spec_test_targets(command)` parses Bash command strings to detect spec test paths targeted by `rm`, `unlink`, `truncate`, redirect-to-empty, `python os.remove`/`os.unlink`/`Path.unlink`, and `mv` to non-archived destinations. The Write tool with empty/whitespace content is also checked as a truncation bypass vector. Escape hatch: `SKIP_SPEC_DELETION_GUARD=1`. Guard fails open when no pipeline context exists (`current_issue == 0`) and on all detection errors. 19 regression tests in `tests/unit/hooks/test_spec_test_deletion_guard.py`. `docs/HOOKS.md` and `docs/HOOK-REGISTRY.md` updated to document the new guard.

- **Planning workflow system: /plan command, plan-critic agent, plan_gate hook** (Issue #814): Adds a structured pre-implementation planning gate. New `/plan` command guides users through a 7-step planning process (problem statement, existing solution search, minimal path design) and saves output to `.claude/plans/`. New `plan-critic` agent (Opus tier) adversarially reviews plans across 5 axes: assumption audit, scope creep detection, existing solution search, minimalism pressure, and uncertainty flagging; requires minimum 2 critique rounds before issuing a PROCEED verdict. New `plan_gate.py` PreToolUse hook enforces plan existence before complex Write/Edit operations — documentation files (`.md`, `.rst`, `.txt`, docs/) and simple edits (<100 lines) are exempt. `plan_validator.py` library validates plan file structure (`## WHY + SCOPE`, `## Existing Solutions`, `## Minimal Path` sections required; plans >72h old trigger warning, not block). New `planning-workflow` skill provides workflow documentation for context injection into plan-critic. Escape hatch: `SKIP_PLAN_CHECK=1`. All gates fail-open. Agent count updated to 16, skill count to 18, command count to 23, hook count to 23.

### Fixed
- **Fix pipeline state file leaking fix-mode into ordering tests** (Issue #847): The `clean_env` autouse fixture in `tests/unit/hooks/test_pipeline_ordering_enforcement.py` previously deleted the `PIPELINE_STATE_FILE` env var rather than redirecting it. When a batch run left `/tmp/implement_pipeline_state.json` on disk with `{"mode": "fix"}`, `_get_pipeline_mode_from_state()` read that file and returned `"fix"` mode — causing `get_required_agents("fix")` to exclude `planner`, which silently allowed the `planner->implementer` prerequisite check to pass even without planner completing. Fix: the fixture now sets `PIPELINE_STATE_FILE` to a nonexistent temp path so the function always falls back to `"full"` mode in tests. 4 regression tests in `TestIssue847PipelineStateIsolation` cover the fallback behavior, correct fix-mode reading when explicitly pointed at a fix-mode state file, and the planner prerequisite enforcement in full mode.

- **Fix batch_issue_number mis-attribution in session_activity_logger** (Issue #808): `_summarize_input()` in `session_activity_logger.py` previously extracted `batch_issue_number` solely by scanning Agent/Task prompt text for the `Issue #N` pattern, which could match incidental issue references unrelated to the batch issue being processed. The extraction now preferentially uses a structured `Issue Number: N` field added to the BATCH CONTEXT block template in `implement-batch.md`, falling back to the `Issue #N` regex only when the structured field is absent (backward compatibility for older prompts). 3 regression tests in `tests/unit/hooks/test_session_activity_logger.py` cover structured field extraction, structured-preferred-over-informal preference, and fallback behavior. `docs/HOOKS.md` updated to document the two-step extraction strategy.

### Added
- **Cluster selector for drain-queue tracker filtering** (Issue #1284): New pure function `select_next_cluster(clusters)` in `plugins/autonomous-dev/lib/cluster_selector.py` filters tracker-shaped issues from drain-queue candidates and prefers their lowest-numbered open leaf children when present. Detects trackers via: title prefix `[TRACKER]`, label combination "root-cause" + "enhancement", or body structure with ≥2 phase headings + ≥2 issue references. When encountering a tracker with open leaves, returns a synthetic single-issue cluster pointing to the lowest leaf. Not yet wired into `/drain-queue` command — integration tracked separately. Added 5 unit tests in `tests/unit/lib/test_cluster_selector.py`.

- **Coordinator bypass verdict tracking** (Issue #1279): Extended `record_plan_critic_skipped()` with optional `bypass_reason` parameter and added new `write_coordinator_bypass_verdict()` function in `pipeline_completion_state.py` to enable drain-queue coordinators to record machine-readable audit trails when bypassing plan-critic for mechanical extensions. New `/drain-queue` command documentation section "Coordinator Bypass of plan-critic" details the two-call protocol. 3 new tests in `tests/regression/test_issue_1279_coordinator_bypass_verdict.py` verify the bypass tracking logic.
- **Hook-level agent completeness gate** (Issue #802): `unified_pre_tool.py` blocks `git commit` during an active pipeline session when required agents have not all completed. `_check_pipeline_agent_completions()` calls `verify_pipeline_agent_completions()` from `pipeline_completion_state.py`, which computes the required agent set from `pipeline_mode` (full/light/fix/tdd-first) and the `research_skipped` flag — so the gate correctly excludes researcher agents when STEP 3.5 determined research could be skipped. Three new functions added to `pipeline_completion_state.py`: `record_research_skipped(session_id, *, issue_number)` records that research was skipped (called by the coordinator after STEP 3.5); `get_research_skipped(session_id, *, issue_number)` checks that flag; `verify_pipeline_agent_completions(session_id, pipeline_mode, *, issue_number)` returns `(passed, completed_agents, missing_agents)`. The `research_skipped` key is persisted to the per-session state file. Fail-open on any state error. Bypass via `SKIP_AGENT_COMPLETENESS_GATE=1`. `implement.md` gains STEP 3.5 (research skip recording) and STEP 9.5 (coordinator-level agent count gate as a defense-in-depth complement to the hook). `docs/LIBRARIES.md` and `docs/HOOK-REGISTRY.md` updated to document the new functions and hook gate. 22 new tests: 15 unit tests in `tests/unit/lib/test_pipeline_completion_state_agent_completeness.py` and 7 regression tests in `tests/regression/test_issue_802_agent_completeness_gate.py`.

- **Enforcement bypass hardening: cross-tool workaround detection and state deletion guard** (Issue #806, consolidating #803 and #804): `unified_pre_tool.py` gains two new bypass-prevention mechanisms. (1) **Cross-tool Write-to-Bash workaround detection** (Issue #803): When Write/Edit to a protected infrastructure file is denied, `_update_deny_cache(file_path)` is now called at both denial points — the main infrastructure protection block and the Issue #750 agent-denial-workaround block — so the denied path is recorded in the deny cache at `/tmp/.claude_deny_cache.jsonl`. Subsequent Bash commands (heredoc, redirect, tee, echo) targeting the same path within the 60-second deny window are detected by a new cross-tool check that calls `_extract_bash_file_writes()` and compares each write target against recently-denied paths via `_check_deny_cache()`; a match within 60 seconds is blocked with a "cross-tool workaround detected" message. (2) **Pipeline state file deletion guard** (Issue #804): New `_check_bash_state_deletion(command)` function detects `rm`, `unlink`, `truncate`, redirect-to-empty, `python os.remove`/`os.unlink`/`Path.unlink` targeting pipeline state files (`/tmp/implement_pipeline_state.json`, `/tmp/.claude_deny_cache.jsonl`, `/tmp/pipeline_completion_state_*.json`, `/tmp/pipeline_secrets/*`) during active pipeline runs. New `_is_state_file(path)` helper checks if a path matches any protected state file pattern. (3) **Enhanced deny cache with basename fallback**: `_check_deny_cache(file_path)` now falls back to matching by `Path.name` when the full path does not match, handling cross-format path references (absolute vs relative). 26 regression and acceptance tests across `tests/regression/test_issue_803_write_bash_workaround.py` (6 tests), `tests/regression/test_issue_804_subagent_ordering_enforcement.py` (11 tests), and `tests/unit/hooks/test_acceptance_806_enforcement_bypass.py` (9 tests). `docs/HOOKS.md` updated to document the new enforcement capabilities.

- **Cumulative batch prompt drift detection and reinvocation-aware threshold relaxation** (Issues #794, #789, #791): `prompt_integrity.py` gains three new functions for cross-issue drift tracking: `record_batch_observation(agent_type, issue_number, word_count)` appends per-invocation word counts to `.claude/logs/prompt_batch_observations.json`; `get_cumulative_shrinkage(agent_type)` returns the percentage drift from the first to the latest observation for an agent (None if fewer than 2 observations); `clear_batch_observations(state_dir=None)` deletes the observations file. `MAX_CUMULATIVE_SHRINKAGE = 0.20` (20%) is the batch-level drift threshold. `REINVOCATION_CONTEXTS = {"remediation", "re-review", "doc-update-retry"}` is a new module-level constant listing invocation types that use relaxed thresholds. `validate_prompt_word_count` gains an `invocation_context: Optional[str]` keyword argument — when the value is in `REINVOCATION_CONTEXTS`, the effective shrinkage threshold is doubled (e.g. 25% → 50%). `clear_prompt_baselines()` now also calls `clear_batch_observations()` so cumulative state resets atomically with baselines at batch start. In `unified_pre_tool.py`: `_detect_invocation_context(prompt)` detects reinvocation context by checking `PIPELINE_INVOCATION_CONTEXT` env var first, then scanning prompt text for markers (`"remediation mode"`, `"re-review"`, `"re_review"`, `"doc-update-retry"`, `"reduced context"`, `"retry with reduced"`); detected context is passed to `validate_prompt_word_count` as `invocation_context`. After each per-issue shrinkage check passes, the hook records a batch observation and checks cumulative drift against `MAX_CUMULATIVE_SHRINKAGE`; exceeding 20% total drift blocks the agent with a REQUIRED NEXT ACTION directive. Both the per-issue relaxed threshold and the cumulative check are wrapped in try/except (fail-open). 26 regression tests across `tests/regression/test_issue_794_progressive_batch_compression.py` (10 tests), `tests/regression/test_issue_789_doc_master_reinvocation.py` (10 tests), and `tests/regression/test_issue_791_remediation_bypass.py` (6 tests). `docs/HOOKS.md`, `docs/LIBRARIES.md`, and `docs/BATCH-PROCESSING.md` updated to document the new enforcement layers.

- **Batch doc-master completion gate in unified_pre_tool.py** (Issue #786): `git commit` in batch worktrees is now blocked when any batch issue is missing `doc-master` completion, mirroring the existing CIA gate (Issue #712). `pipeline_completion_state.py` gains `verify_batch_doc_master_completions(session_id)` which checks completion state for `doc-master` across all tracked batch issues (skips issue key `"0"` for non-batch runs), returns `(all_passed, issues_with_doc_master, issues_missing_doc_master)`, and fails open on any state error. `unified_pre_tool.py` calls `_check_batch_doc_master_completions()` before allowing `git commit` in batch worktrees — identical structure to `_check_batch_cia_completions()`. Block message includes a `REQUIRED NEXT ACTION` directing the coordinator to run doc-master for missing issues. Bypass via `SKIP_BATCH_DOC_MASTER_GATE=1`. Regression coverage: 21 new test functions across `tests/unit/hooks/test_batch_doc_master_gate.py` (8 tests), `tests/unit/lib/test_pipeline_completion_state_doc_master.py` (8 tests), and additions to `tests/unit/hooks/test_pipeline_ordering_enforcement.py` (5 tests). `docs/HOOKS.md` and `docs/LIBRARIES.md` updated to document the gate and the new `verify_batch_doc_master_completions()` function. `pipeline_completion_state.py` version bumped to v1.2.0.

- **AST-based smoke test for tautological `assert True` assertions** (Issue #783): New `tests/regression/smoke/test_tautological_assertions.py` scans all test files using Python's `ast` module for test functions whose sole assertion is a bare `assert True` (no message), which can never fail and provides zero verification value. Uses an explicit allowlist (`ALLOWLIST`) for 22 pre-existing tautological tests tracked under Issue #783 for future cleanup. A companion test `test_allowlist_entries_still_exist` enforces allowlist hygiene by failing if an allowlisted entry is fixed but the entry is not removed. `assert True, "reason"` (with a message) and functions containing `pytest.raises`/`mock.assert_*` verification calls are correctly excluded from findings.

- **reviewer agent `assert True` tautological assertion detection** (Issue #783): The reviewer's **Testing** taxonomy checklist in `plugins/autonomous-dev/agents/reviewer.md` is extended to explicitly direct the reviewer to scan for `assert True` tautological assertions during code review — a test whose only assertion is `assert True` can never fail and provides zero verification value. The import-as-test pattern does not need `assert True`; import failure is the test signal.

- **Auto-create Dependabot security issues during /implement STEP 13** (Issue #767): New `dependabot_tracker.py` library queries the GitHub Dependabot API for open vulnerability alerts after each `git push` and auto-creates deduplicated tracking issues without blocking the pipeline. Critical and high severity alerts each get an individual issue titled `[Security] Dependabot: {package} {GHSA_ID}` with a severity label; medium severity alerts are batched into one issue per ISO calendar week. Deduplication is enforced via HTML comment markers (`<!-- dependabot-ghsa: GHSA-... -->` and `<!-- dependabot-medium-batch: YYYY-WNN -->`) so re-running the tracker never creates duplicate issues. GHSA ID validation (`GHSA-xxxx-xxxx-xxxx` lowercase alphanumeric) guards all issue creation paths. The `_gh()` subprocess wrapper uses `shell=False` throughout. `implement.md` STEP 13 gains the tracker invocation block positioned after `git push` and before `gh issue close`, wrapped in `try/except` and `2>/dev/null || true` for complete non-blocking behavior. 49 tests across `tests/unit/lib/test_dependabot_tracker.py` (34 unit tests covering URL parsing, GHSA validation, alert fetching, issue deduplication, issue creation, medium batch handling, entry point orchestration, and security invariants) and `tests/unit/lib/test_acceptance_dependabot_tracker.py` (15 static file inspection acceptance tests). `docs/LIBRARIES.md` and `docs/ARCHITECTURE-OVERVIEW.md` updated to document the new library. Library count incremented to 186.

- **Expand Hypothesis property-based testing to 13 files covering all pure lib functions** (Issue #769): Property test suite grows from 3 to 13 files. Ten new test files added: `test_validation_properties.py`, `test_failure_classifier_properties.py`, `test_prompt_integrity_properties.py`, `test_tool_approval_audit_properties.py`, `test_quality_enforcer_properties.py`, `test_runtime_aggregator_properties.py`, `test_auto_approval_engine_properties.py`, `test_settings_generator_properties.py`, `test_acceptance_criteria_parser_properties.py`, `test_math_utils_properties.py` — covering serialize/deserialize roundtrips, input validation invariants, set operations, scoring functions, and mathematical identities. Three existing files (`test_pipeline_state_properties.py`, `test_security_utils_properties.py`, `test_tool_validator_properties.py`) updated with module-level strategy extraction and `@example` edge case decorators. `tests/property/conftest.py` gains Hypothesis profile configuration: `default` profile (`max_examples=50` for fast local iteration) and `ci` profile (`max_examples=200`, `deadline=None`, `suppress_health_check=[HealthCheck.too_slow]` for thorough CI runs); select via `HYPOTHESIS_PROFILE=ci`. `plugins/autonomous-dev/skills/testing-guide/SKILL.md` updated with PBT Candidate Selection section documenting good/bad candidates and strategy rules. `docs/TESTING-STRATEGY.md` Layer 3 updated to list all 13 property test files and correct the `max_examples` description to reflect profile-based configuration.

- **Analytic rubric and consistency-check patterns for GenAI LLM-as-judge tests**: `GenAIClient` in `tests/genai/conftest.py` gains two new judge methods: `judge_analytic()` evaluates content against a list of per-criterion dicts (each with `name`, `description`, `max_points`), calling the LLM once per criterion for independent MET/UNMET binary judgment and returning `{"criteria_results", "total_score", "max_score", "pass", "band", "reasoning"}`; `judge_consistent()` runs N evaluation rounds (default 3) with cache-busting, uses the median score as the final result, and returns `{"rounds", "agreement", "scores", "final_score", "pass", "band", "reasoning"}`. `ask()` gains an explicit `temperature` keyword argument defaulting to `0` for deterministic, reproducible judging. 5 existing GenAI tests migrated to analytic rubric (`test_security_posture.py`, `test_hard_gate_enforcement.py`, `test_hook_coverage.py`, `test_error_handling.py`) and consistency-check (`test_pipeline_completeness.py`) patterns as reference examples. `plugins/autonomous-dev/skills/testing-guide/SKILL.md` updated with Analytic Rubric Pattern, Consistency Check Pattern, and Temperature Guidance sections. `docs/TESTING-STRATEGY.md` Layer 5 updated to document all four `GenAIClient` judge methods and revised best-practice bullets. 21 unit tests in `tests/unit/genai/test_genai_client.py` cover all three judge methods, temperature caching, cache key behavior, and JSON extraction edge cases.

- **Spec-blind validation step in /implement pipeline** (STEP 8.5, HARD GATE): New `spec-validator` agent (Opus tier) validates implementation against acceptance criteria without seeing implementation details, providing an independent verification layer that cannot be biased by knowledge of how the code works. The agent receives ONLY acceptance criteria, feature description, and changed file paths — implementer output, code diffs, reviewer feedback, research findings, and planner rationale are explicitly forbidden. PASS proceeds to validation; FAIL triggers implementer remediation (max 2 cycles). Same isolation applied in `/implement --fix` as STEP F3.5. `batch_agent_verifier.py` updated to include `spec-validator` in the required-agent set (default pipeline now requires 8 agents: researcher-local, researcher, planner, implementer, spec-validator, reviewer, security-auditor, doc-master). `known_bypass_patterns.json` updated to detect spec-validator step skips. `install_manifest.json` updated to register `spec-validator.md`. `skills/testing-guide/SKILL.md` updated with spec-blind testing guidance. Agent count increased from 14 to 15. 27 regression tests across `tests/regression/smoke/test_spec_validator_agent.py` (12 tests) and `tests/regression/smoke/test_spec_validator_pipeline.py` (15 tests) cover context boundary enforcement, PASS/FAIL verdict paths, remediation triggering, and pipeline integration. `docs/AGENTS.md` and `docs/ARCHITECTURE-OVERVIEW.md` updated to reflect the new agent and pipeline step. `CLAUDE.md` component count updated to 15 agents.

- **SQLite index for conversation archiver** (Issue #773): `conversation_archiver.py` now writes session metadata to `~/.claude/archive/sessions.db` (Python stdlib `sqlite3`) alongside the existing JSONL index at `~/.claude/archive/index.jsonl`. The SQLite index started as a 15-column session record (session_id, project, cwd, archive_path, first_seen, last_updated, message counts, token totals, model, first_user_prompt) — extended to 17 columns in a later fix to capture `total_cache_read_tokens` and `total_cache_creation_tokens`. Upsert-on-session_id semantics preserve the original `first_seen` timestamp across repeated Stop events. The JSONL index is preserved unchanged for jq/grep compatibility. No new external dependencies. 6 unit tests in `tests/unit/hooks/test_conversation_archiver.py` (`TestSqliteIndex`) cover happy path, upsert preserving first_seen, table auto-creation, error resilience, queryability, and end-to-end integration through `main()`. `docs/HOOKS.md` and `docs/HOOK-REGISTRY.md` updated to document both index paths.

- **Global Stop hook archives conversation transcripts for pattern analysis** (Issue #773): New `conversation_archiver.py` Stop hook writes complete Claude Code conversation transcripts to `~/.claude/archive/` on every Stop event, enabling long-term pattern analysis across sessions. Pure Python stdlib, non-blocking, always exits 0. Registered in `global_settings_template.json` and all six settings templates. Sidecar `conversation_archiver.hook.json` declares Stop lifecycle registration. 38 unit tests in `tests/unit/hooks/test_conversation_archiver.py`.

### Fixed
- **Register conversation_archiver.hook.json in install manifest** (Issue #787): `plugins/autonomous-dev/config/install_manifest.json` was missing the sidecar entry `"plugins/autonomous-dev/hooks/conversation_archiver.hook.json"` from its hooks files array. The `.hook.json` file was written to disk in the original Issue #773 commit (33adbc7) but the corresponding manifest registration was omitted, meaning `/setup` and `/sync` would not deploy the sidecar alongside the hook script. The fix adds the missing entry immediately before the existing `conversation_archiver.py` line. No new tests needed — the pre-existing `tests/unit/hooks/test_conversation_archiver.py::TestHookRegistration::test_hook_in_install_manifest` was the failing test that caught the regression and now passes.

- **File-based fallback for PIPELINE_ISSUE_NUMBER in hook invocations** (Issue #779): `_get_current_issue_number()` helper added to both `unified_pre_tool.py` and `unified_session_tracker.py`. Env vars set via `export` inside a Bash tool call do not persist to subsequent hook invocations because each Bash call runs in a fresh shell and hooks inherit env from the Claude Code parent process, not from prior Bash sessions. As a result, the ordering gate and prompt integrity gate were reading `PIPELINE_ISSUE_NUMBER` as `"0"` and recording/checking agent completions under the wrong issue number in batch mode, causing false-positive ordering blocks between batch issues. The helper resolves the issue number in two steps: (1) `PIPELINE_ISSUE_NUMBER` env var when it is set and non-zero, (2) the `issue_number` field in `/tmp/implement_pipeline_state.json` written by the coordinator, (3) safe default of `0`. `docs/BATCH-PROCESSING.md` updated to document the two-step resolution. 12 regression tests in `tests/regression/test_issue_779_env_var_fallback.py`.

- **Per-issue prompt baseline isolation in batch mode** (Issue #764): `get_prompt_baseline()` in `prompt_integrity.py` now accepts an optional `issue_number` keyword argument. When provided, it returns the baseline for that specific issue only, preventing a longer prompt from one issue seeding the baseline that then blocks a legitimately shorter prompt in a later issue (cross-issue false-positive shrinkage block). `unified_pre_tool.py` reads `PIPELINE_ISSUE_NUMBER` env var and passes it to `get_prompt_baseline()` so each batch issue compares only against its own recorded baseline. When `PIPELINE_ISSUE_NUMBER` is absent (non-batch mode), `issue_number=None` preserves the original lowest-issue backward-compatible behavior. `docs/LIBRARIES.md` and `docs/BATCH-PROCESSING.md` updated to document the new parameter. 6 regression tests in `tests/regression/test_issue_764_per_issue_baseline.py` cover per-issue isolation, within-issue shrinkage detection, backward compatibility, baseline seeding with current issue number, multi-issue full isolation, and the JSON structure of per-issue baselines. 3 pre-existing test failures in `tests/regression/test_issue_748_template_baseline.py` fixed by correcting assertions to account for the 0.70 slack factor applied by `seed_baselines_from_templates()`.

- **Block Python -c settings file write bypass in unified_pre_tool.py** (Issue #768): `_detect_settings_json_write()` previously only checked shell redirects, `tee`, `sed -i`, and `cp`/`mv` patterns. A Bash command like `python3 -c "p='settings.json'; json.dump(d, open(p,'w'))"` bypassed detection entirely because `_extract_bash_file_writes` only matched literal paths in `open()` calls, not variable indirection. Fixed by adding a new detection block that extracts `python3 -c` / `python -c` inline snippets and checks whether they contain both a settings file reference (`settings.json` or `settings.local.json`) and a Python write pattern (`open(`, `json.dump(`, `.write(`, `.write_text(`, `shutil.`, `os.rename(`, `os.replace(`). The bypass was exploited within 33 seconds of a blocked Edit attempt by switching to a Bash+Python workaround. 17 regression tests in `tests/regression/smoke/test_python_settings_bypass.py` cover 8 blocking cases, 6 allow cases, and 3 existing-detection-verification cases.

- **Fix test isolation in test_critical_agent_allowed_outside_pipeline_when_adequate** (Issue #784): Added `patch("prompt_integrity.get_prompt_baseline", return_value=None)` to prevent on-disk baseline state from causing false denials when a prior session has seeded a high baseline. Without the mock, a 100-word prompt would be denied at 86.9% shrinkage if the on-disk baseline was 763 words (above the 25% threshold). One regression test `test_baseline_isolation_prevents_false_denial` added to `tests/unit/hooks/test_prompt_integrity_enforcement.py` to prove the mock is load-bearing by explicitly verifying both that None baseline allows and that a baseline of 763 denies.

- **Remove unbalanced-parenthesis deny rules from DEFAULT_DENY_LIST** (`settings_generator.py`): Two shell-injection deny rules — `Bash(*$(rm*)` and `` Bash(*`rm*) `` — contained unbalanced parentheses that caused Claude Code's glob parser to skip the entire `settings.json` file silently, disabling all hooks and permissions. Both patterns have been removed. The attack vectors they targeted (command substitution injection) remain covered by the existing `Bash(*|*sh*)` and `Bash(*|*bash*)` pipe-based rules. `docs/TOOL-AUTO-APPROVAL.md` updated to remove the stale documentation of these two patterns from both the example deny list and the blacklist categories table.

- **Fix invalid glob deny rules that silently disabled all settings** (`settings_generator.py`): 15 deny rules in `DEFAULT_DENY_LIST` used invalid `Bash(command:*pattern*)` colon syntax where the pattern did not end in `:*`. Claude Code's tool permission parser requires the `command:` prefix syntax to have `:*` at the END of the pattern string; placing additional pattern text after `:*` (e.g., `Bash(git:*--force*)`) caused the rule to be silently skipped, rendering all 15 deny rules ineffective. Patterns converted to glob syntax: `Bash(curl:*|*sh*)` → `Bash(*curl *|*sh*)`, `Bash(git:*--force*)` → `Bash(*git *--force*)`, `Bash(apt:*install*)` → `Bash(*apt *install*)`, and 12 similar rules covering wget, git push/reset/clean, apt/yum/brew remove, npm global install. `docs/LIBRARIES.md` `build_deny_list()` examples updated to reflect the corrected patterns. 3 regression tests added in `tests/regression/smoke/test_permission_glob_syntax.py` covering `:*` placement validation.

### Fixed
- **Worktree-aware log directory resolution in session_activity_logger** (Issue #755): `_find_log_dir()` in `session_activity_logger.py` now detects when the hook's CWD is inside a `.worktrees/` subdirectory and resolves to the parent repository's `.claude/logs/activity/` path via `git rev-parse --path-format=absolute --git-common-dir`. Previously, hooks running inside a worktree wrote activity logs to the worktree's own `.claude/logs/activity/` directory (or fell back to CWD), so downstream agent events were siloed away from main-session events and missed by post-session analysis tools such as `continuous-improvement-analyst` and `pipeline_intent_validator`. The fix ensures all events from a batch session — whether emitted from the main repo or a worktree — land in the same JSONL file. Falls through to normal walk-up resolution on any git subprocess error or when the resolved parent `.claude/` directory does not exist. `docs/HOOKS.md` `session_activity_logger` entry updated to document the worktree-aware resolution behavior.

- **Restore `_detect_realign_bypass` enforcement in unified_pre_tool.py** (Issue #754): `_detect_realign_bypass(tool_name, tool_input)` was accidentally removed in a prior refactor and has been restored. The function blocks Bash commands that directly invoke `python -m mlx_lm.lora`, `python -m mlx_lm.fuse`, `python -m mlx_lm.generate`, `python -m mlx_lm`, or `python -m mlx.launch` in realign projects. Project detection checks for `src/realign/` directory or "realign" string in `pyproject.toml`; grep/search/cat references to mlx_lm are explicitly allowed and not blocked. Block message directs the user to use `realign train` or `realign generate` instead. Fails open on all project-detection errors. `docs/HOOKS.md` and `docs/HOOK-REGISTRY.md` unified_pre_tool entries updated to document the realign CLI enforcement. 14 regression tests in `tests/unit/hooks/test_realign_bypass_detection.py` cover: mlx_lm.lora blocked, mlx.launch blocked, mlx_lm.generate blocked, mlx_lm.fuse blocked, python3 variant blocked, realign train allowed, non-Bash tool allowed, grep search allowed, rg search allowed, cat reference allowed, empty command allowed, regular python command allowed, function importability, and return type verification.

### Changed
- **Deprecate template-based baseline seeding in favor of first-observation seeding** (Issue #810): `seed_baselines_from_templates()` in `prompt_integrity.py` is now a no-op that logs a deprecation warning and returns an empty dict. Template files (~2500 words) are far larger than coordinator-constructed task-specific prompts (~200-600 words); even the prior 0.70× slack factor left template-derived baselines at ~1700 words, causing a systematic 25-50% false positive block rate on legitimate batch invocations. The correct approach — already implemented in the hook's else-branch since Issue #759 — is first-observation seeding: `clear_prompt_baselines()` resets state at batch start and the first observed prompt for each agent per issue automatically becomes the baseline. `implement-batch.md` STEP B3 updated to remove the `seed_baselines_from_templates()` call. Test expectations in `tests/unit/lib/test_prompt_integrity.py` and `tests/regression/test_issue_759_template_baseline_slack.py` updated to reflect the no-op behavior (3 test functions replaced, net 0 change). `docs/LIBRARIES.md` and `docs/BATCH-PROCESSING.md` updated to document the deprecated function and the observation-based seeding approach.

- **Relax post-hoc progressive compression threshold from 25% to 40%** (Issue #757): `MAX_PROMPT_SHRINKAGE_RATIO` in `pipeline_intent_validator.py` raised from `0.25` to `0.40`. The post-hoc check (run after the batch completes via `detect_progressive_compression()`) was firing false positives because legitimate task-level prompt variation between batch issues exceeds 25%. The real-time hook in `unified_pre_tool.py` continues to enforce 25% shrinkage per-invocation; this post-hoc threshold is intentionally more generous to catch only severe drift patterns. `docs/BATCH-PROCESSING.md` updated to reflect the new 40% threshold and clarify the distinction from the hook-level threshold. 3 updated tests in `tests/unit/lib/test_prompt_compression_detection.py` cover: 30% compression not flagged at new threshold, exact 40% boundary behavior, and 39% passing.

### Fixed
- **Document undocumented proxy file path in verify_state_hmac stale fail-open** (Issue #761): `pipeline_state.py` extracts the previously hardcoded `Path("/tmp/implement_pipeline_state.json")` into a module-level constant `LEGACY_SENTINEL_PATH: Path` with an 8-line comment block explaining its role as the legacy sentinel file (distinct from the HMAC-signed per-run state file at `/tmp/pipeline_state_{run_id}.json`), why its mtime is used as the stale-detection proxy, and cross-references to `unified_pre_tool.py`'s `PIPELINE_STATE_FILE` env var. `verify_state_hmac()` now references `LEGACY_SENTINEL_PATH` instead of constructing an inline path. Enhanced inline comments in the stale fail-open block explain that the sentinel mtime cannot be forged by editing JSON content (unlike `session_start` in the state dict). No behavioral change — same path, same threshold, same fail-open logic. 3 regression tests added to `tests/regression/test_issue_753_hmac_stale_failopen.py` (`TestIssue761SentinelPathPinning`): `test_legacy_sentinel_path_constant_exists` pins the constant value, `test_legacy_sentinel_path_is_not_hmac_state_path` asserts no collision with per-run state paths, and `test_verify_state_hmac_checks_legacy_sentinel_path` verifies the constant is the path interrogated during verification. `docs/LIBRARIES.md` entry updated to document the new constant.

- **HMAC stale fail-open for batch/worktree sessions** (Issue #753): `verify_state_hmac()` in `pipeline_state.py` now returns True for pipeline state files whose `session_start` timestamp is older than 1 hour when HMAC verification fails (secret file absent). In batch and worktree workflows, `/clear` cleans up the per-run secret file between issues; a leftover state file from a prior session then fails HMAC on every subsequent hook invocation, producing up to 99 block events per session. The fix treats secret-absent states older than 1 hour as expired rather than tampered, so enforcement falls through to the normal no-state path instead of blocking. States with a valid HMAC still pass immediately regardless of age; fresh states (<1 hour) with an unverifiable HMAC still return False. `docs/LIBRARIES.md` entry for `pipeline_state.py` updated to document the stale fail-open behavior. 4 regression tests in `tests/regression/test_issue_753_hmac_stale_failopen.py` cover: stale state with invalid HMAC passes, fresh state with invalid HMAC still blocks, missing session_start with invalid HMAC blocks, and valid HMAC passes regardless of age.

- **Step ordering validator false positive in mixed-mode batches** (Issue #760): `_validate_step_ordering_for_group` in `pipeline_intent_validator.py` previously used an `all()` check to skip the planner→implementer ordering test when every implementer event was `--fix` mode. In mixed-mode batches containing both a `--fix` issue (implementer only, no planner) and a full-pipeline issue (implementer correctly after planner), the `all()` check would fail because not all implementers were `--fix`, then compare the earliest implementer timestamp — which belonged to the `--fix` issue — against the planner, producing a false CRITICAL `step_ordering` finding. Fixed by filtering out `--fix` implementers before the timestamp comparison rather than requiring all implementers to be `--fix`. The check now skips only when zero non-fix implementers remain, and fires only when a non-fix implementer genuinely precedes the planner. 3 regression tests in `tests/regression/test_issue_760_mixed_mode_ordering.py`.

- **Fix-mode pipeline validation: planner exemption and missing security-auditor detection** (Issues #788, #798, consolidated as #801): `pipeline_intent_validator.py` gains two new constants and a new detection function. `PLANNER_EXEMPT_MODES = {"--fix", "fix"}` lists pipeline modes that intentionally skip the planner step. `_validate_step_ordering_for_group` now performs group-level mode detection: it collects the set of `pipeline_mode` values across all events in the group and, when any intersect with `PLANNER_EXEMPT_MODES`, removes the `("planner", "implementer")` pair from enforcement entirely — replacing the prior per-event filtering approach and preventing false CRITICAL `step_ordering` findings in fix-mode and batch-fix-mode pipeline runs. Empty or unknown `pipeline_mode` defaults to full enforcement as a fail-safe. `SECURITY_SENSITIVE_PATTERNS` constant (tuple) covers five path substrings: `hooks/`, `lib/auto_approval_engine`, `lib/tool_validator`, `config/auto_approve_policy`, `lib/security`. `detect_missing_security_review(log_path, events, *, session_id)` scans the JSONL log for Write/Edit tool entries targeting any of these paths; if matches are found and no `security-auditor` event exists in the session, a WARNING finding with `finding_type="missing_security_review"` is emitted. Test files (`tests/` prefix) are excluded from triggering the warning. `PipelineEvent.pipeline_mode` field (str) is now populated from `pipeline_mode` in JSONL `input_summary` entries. 11 new tests: 6 in `TestFixModeStepOrdering` and 5 in `TestMissingSecurityReview` in `tests/unit/lib/test_pipeline_intent_validator.py`; smoke regression tests in `tests/regression/smoke/test_pipeline_fix_mode_validation.py`.

- **Prompt integrity false positives from template-based baseline seeding** (Issues #759, #756): `unified_pre_tool.py` no-baseline branch now seeds from the **observed prompt word count** instead of the agent template file word count. Template files (~2500 words) are far larger than coordinator-constructed task-specific prompts (~200-400 words), and even the 0.70 slack factor applied in `seed_baselines_from_templates()` left template-derived baselines (~1700 words) too high, blocking legitimate first-issue invocations. The hook's `else` branch now calls `record_prompt_baseline(agent_type, issue_number=0, word_count=word_count)` directly with the observed count. `prompt_integrity.py` `seed_baselines_from_templates()` retains the 0.70 slack factor for batch-mode pre-seeding (called from `implement-batch.md` STEP B3) where it compares across issues rather than against single task-specific prompts. Updated test expectations in `tests/unit/hooks/test_validate_prompt_integrity_shrinkage.py` and `tests/unit/lib/test_prompt_integrity.py`. 4 regression tests in `tests/regression/test_issue_759_template_baseline_slack.py` verify the 0.70 slack factor application in `seed_baselines_from_templates()`, the false-positive scenario is eliminated, genuinely compressed prompts still fail, and the hook seeds from observed count. `docs/HOOKS.md` Prompt Integrity Gate bullet updated to describe the observed-count seeding behavior.

### Added
- **Cluster selector for drain-queue tracker filtering** (Issue #1284): New pure function `select_next_cluster(clusters)` in `plugins/autonomous-dev/lib/cluster_selector.py` filters tracker-shaped issues from drain-queue candidates and prefers their lowest-numbered open leaf children when present. Detects trackers via: title prefix `[TRACKER]`, label combination "root-cause" + "enhancement", or body structure with ≥2 phase headings + ≥2 issue references. When encountering a tracker with open leaves, returns a synthetic single-issue cluster pointing to the lowest leaf. Not yet wired into `/drain-queue` command — integration tracked separately. Added 5 unit tests in `tests/unit/lib/test_cluster_selector.py`.

- **Coordinator bypass verdict tracking** (Issue #1279): Extended `record_plan_critic_skipped()` with optional `bypass_reason` parameter and added new `write_coordinator_bypass_verdict()` function in `pipeline_completion_state.py` to enable drain-queue coordinators to record machine-readable audit trails when bypassing plan-critic for mechanical extensions. New `/drain-queue` command documentation section "Coordinator Bypass of plan-critic" details the two-call protocol. 3 new tests in `tests/regression/test_issue_1279_coordinator_bypass_verdict.py` verify the bypass tracking logic.
- **Test pruning tooling and CI gate** (Issue #736): `test_pruning_analyzer.py` gains `prune_tests(dry_run=True, categories=None, exclude_dirs=None) -> PruneResult` — deletes fully-flagged test files with safety guards: dry-run default, security tests excluded (`tests/security/`), only safe categories (`dead_import`, `archived_ref`, `zero_assertion`), whole-file-only deletion (all test functions must be flagged), and tier protection (T0/T1 files never deleted). `PruneResult` dataclass carries `deleted_files`, `skipped_files`, `dry_run`, and `error_messages`. `commands/sweep.md` gains `--prune` flag: `/sweep --tests --prune` runs pruner with `dry_run=False`; `/sweep --tests --prune --dry-run` shows candidates without deleting. `test_lifecycle_manager.py` gains `check_prunable_threshold(report, threshold=None) -> (bool, str)` CI gate (default threshold: 100 prunable findings) and module-level `PRUNABLE_THRESHOLD = 100` constant; `format_dashboard()` now includes a Gate Status line showing PASS/FAIL. `docs/LIBRARIES.md` detailed entry for `test_pruning_analyzer.py` corrected: removed stale "Never auto-deletes files" claim, added `prune_tests()` and `PruneResult` to the Public API section. 9 unit tests in `tests/unit/lib/test_test_pruning_analyzer.py` cover `PruneResult` dataclass fields, dry-run behavior, actual deletion, partial-flag skipping, security directory exclusion, tier protection, safe-categories-only filtering, deletion error handling, and empty-findings early return.

### Security
- **Sanitize session_id in agent denial state file path construction** (Issue #752): `unified_pre_tool.py` `_record_agent_denial()` and `_check_agent_denial()` used unsanitized `_session_id` in `os.path.join()` for state file paths, allowing path traversal via crafted session IDs containing `../` sequences (CWE-22, OWASP A01:2021). Added `_sanitize_session_id()` with 4-layer defense-in-depth: null byte stripping, Unicode NFKC normalization, allowlist regex (`[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]` → `_`), and 128-char length cap. Sanitization applied at point of assignment in `main()`. Path confinement checks (`os.path.realpath()` + `startswith()`) added to both functions as defense-in-depth. Replaced predictable `.tmp` suffix with `tempfile.mkstemp()` for atomic creation (prevents symlink/TOCTOU attacks). Fail-open contract preserved. 8 unit tests in `tests/unit/hooks/test_agent_denial_blocking.py`; 5 security tests in `tests/security/test_session_id_sanitization.py`; 10 acceptance tests in `tests/unit/hooks/test_session_id_sanitization_acceptance.py`.

### Fixed
- **Shrinkage gate test isolation in test_prompt_integrity_enforcement.py** (Issue #747): `test_critical_agent_above_minimum_allowed` was failing because Issue #723 added baseline shrinkage gate logic to `validate_prompt_integrity()`, and the test's prompt was below the stored baseline, causing an unexpected deny. The test now patches `get_prompt_baseline` to return `None` so it exercises only the minimum word count gate. Two new tests cover the shrinkage gate explicitly: `test_critical_agent_shrinkage_detected` verifies the shrinkage gate deny path when the prompt has shrunk beyond the 25% threshold, and `test_critical_agent_no_shrinkage_when_above_baseline` verifies the allow path when the prompt remains within threshold. This is a test-only fix — no production code was changed.

### Fixed
- **Template-derived baseline seeding prevents first-issue compression corrupting the shrinkage gate** (Issue #748): `prompt_integrity.py` gains two new public functions: `compute_template_baselines(agents_dir=None)` reads each critical agent's `.md` template file and returns `{agent_type: word_count}` for all agents with found templates; `seed_baselines_from_templates(agents_dir=None, state_dir=None)` seeds `prompt_baselines.json` with those template-derived word counts at `issue_number=0`. Previously the shrinkage gate used the first agent invocation's observed word count as the baseline, which could already be compressed (root cause of `-46%` implementer and `-49%` security-auditor silent regressions across batch runs). `unified_pre_tool.py` no-baseline fallback branch now calls `get_agent_prompt_template()` and seeds from template word count instead of the observed prompt word count; falls back to observed count only if the template file is missing. `implement-batch.md` STEP B3 updated to call `seed_baselines_from_templates()` immediately after `clear_prompt_baselines()` at batch start. `docs/LIBRARIES.md` entry 73 updated to document the two new functions and correct the library default shrinkage threshold (15%, not 20%). `docs/BATCH-PROCESSING.md` "How prompt_integrity.py Works" section updated to replace the first-issue observed seeding step with template seeding at batch start. `docs/HOOKS.md` Prompt Integrity Gate description updated to describe the template-first fallback. 11 tests: 5 unit tests in `tests/unit/lib/test_prompt_integrity.py` (`TestTemplateBaselineSeeding`), 6 regression tests in `tests/regression/test_issue_748_template_baseline.py`.

### Added
- **Cluster selector for drain-queue tracker filtering** (Issue #1284): New pure function `select_next_cluster(clusters)` in `plugins/autonomous-dev/lib/cluster_selector.py` filters tracker-shaped issues from drain-queue candidates and prefers their lowest-numbered open leaf children when present. Detects trackers via: title prefix `[TRACKER]`, label combination "root-cause" + "enhancement", or body structure with ≥2 phase headings + ≥2 issue references. When encountering a tracker with open leaves, returns a synthetic single-issue cluster pointing to the lowest leaf. Not yet wired into `/drain-queue` command — integration tracked separately. Added 5 unit tests in `tests/unit/lib/test_cluster_selector.py`.

- **Coordinator bypass verdict tracking** (Issue #1279): Extended `record_plan_critic_skipped()` with optional `bypass_reason` parameter and added new `write_coordinator_bypass_verdict()` function in `pipeline_completion_state.py` to enable drain-queue coordinators to record machine-readable audit trails when bypassing plan-critic for mechanical extensions. New `/drain-queue` command documentation section "Coordinator Bypass of plan-critic" details the two-call protocol. 3 new tests in `tests/regression/test_issue_1279_coordinator_bypass_verdict.py` verify the bypass tracking logic.
- **Shallow verdict detection fields in DocVerdictResult** (Issue #758): `doc_verdict_validator.py` gains `word_count` (int) and `is_shallow` (bool) fields on `DocVerdictResult`, plus a module-level `MIN_DOC_VERDICT_WORDS = 100` constant. `validate_doc_verdict()` now populates these fields on every return path — empty input returns `word_count=0, is_shallow=True`; non-empty input computes word count via `split()` and sets `is_shallow = word_count < MIN_DOC_VERDICT_WORDS`. Coordinators consuming `DocVerdictResult` can now inspect `is_shallow` rather than re-implementing the word count check. 6 regression tests in `tests/regression/test_issue_758_doc_verdict_shallow.py` cover: short output flagged as shallow, adequate output not flagged, empty output shallow, exact 100-word boundary passes, 99-word boundary fails, and `MIN_DOC_VERDICT_WORDS` constant value.

- **doc-master minimum output HARD GATE and shallow verdict detection** (Issue #744): `doc-master.md` gains Step 4.6, a 100-word minimum output HARD GATE — responses under 100 words are evidence that the `covers:` scan or semantic comparison was skipped. The doc-master agent prompt explicitly forbids sub-100-word responses and instructs the agent to expand by listing docs checked, what was compared, and why each affected doc passed or required a fix. `implement-fix.md` STEP F4 and `implement-batch.md` STEP B3 both gain Shallow Verdict Detection: after each doc-master invocation, the coordinator counts the output words; if fewer than 100, it logs `[DOC-VERDICT-SHALLOW] doc-master produced N words (minimum: 100)` and retries once with reduced context. If the retry also produces fewer than 100 words, the coordinator records `doc-drift-verdict: SHALLOW` with a warning. The `doc-drift-verdict` display in per-issue agent verification extended from `PASS/FAIL/MISSING` to `PASS/FAIL/MISSING/SHALLOW`. `docs/AGENTS.md` doc-master entry updated to document the minimum output enforcement and token count updated to reflect prompt growth. 2 regression tests in `tests/regression/test_issue_744_doc_master_shallow_verdict.py`.

### Fixed
- **Block coordinator Write/Edit fallback after prompt-shrinkage agent denial** (Issue #750): When `unified_pre_tool.py` denies an Agent/Task call via the Prompt Integrity Gate, the orchestrator could fall back to direct Write/Edit calls to the same protected infrastructure files. Added `_record_agent_denial(agent_type)` which writes a session-scoped JSON state file at `/tmp/adev-agent-deny-{session_id}.json` immediately after any prompt-integrity denial; added `_check_agent_denial()` which checks for a denial record within `AGENT_DENY_TTL` (300 seconds) before allowing Write/Edit to protected infrastructure paths. Block message includes a `REQUIRED NEXT ACTION` directive telling the orchestrator to reload the agent prompt template via `get_agent_prompt_template()` and retry the Agent call with a full-length prompt. Non-infrastructure paths (docs, config, test files) are unaffected. Both functions fail open on all state file errors. `docs/HOOKS.md` gains an "Agent Denial Fallback Guard" section; `docs/HOOK-REGISTRY.md` unified_pre_tool entry updated to document the guard. 16 unit tests in `tests/unit/hooks/test_agent_denial_blocking.py`; 3 regression tests in `tests/regression/test_issue_750_agent_denial_fallback.py`.

### Fixed
- **CIA missing from --fix and --light pipeline agent sets** (Issue #751): `continuous-improvement-analyst` was absent from `FIX_PIPELINE_AGENTS` and `LIGHT_PIPELINE_AGENTS` in `agent_ordering_gate.py`, causing the ordering gate to allow --fix and --light pipeline runs to complete without CIA. Both sets now include CIA, ensuring the agent ordering gate and batch completeness checks enforce its presence. `implement-fix.md` gains STEP F5 (Continuous Improvement) with a FORBIDDEN gate against skipping it; agent count corrected from 3-4 to 4-5. `implement.md` light pipeline updated to launch CIA before cleanup. 6 regression tests added in `tests/unit/lib/test_agent_ordering_gate.py`.

Detects trackers via: title prefix `[TRACKER]`, label combination "root-cause" + "enhancement", or body structure with ≥2 phase headings + ≥2 issue references. - **CHANGELOG scope boundary enforcement for doc-master** (Issue #741): `doc-master.md` gains a "CHANGELOG Scope Boundary" section preventing the agent from folding prior-commit documentation gaps into the current commit's CHANGELOG entry. Before writing any CHANGELOG bullet, doc-master now verifies the described change traces back to a file in `git diff --name-only`. Prior-commit drift discovered mid-run is reported as a `DOC-DRIFT-FOUND (prior commit): [description] — belongs to commit [sha]` finding, and a standalone doc-fix commit is recommended instead of silent attribution. Three FORBIDDEN rules enforce no cross-commit attribution, no silent folding, and no ignored prior-commit gaps. Regression test in `tests/regression/test_issue_741_doc_master_changelog_scope.py`. `docs/AGENTS.md` doc-master entry updated to document the scope boundary behavior.

- **Cross-repo finding routing for continuous-improvement-analyst** (Issue #739): The CIA agent now annotates each finding with a `target_repo` field (`autonomous-dev`, `consumer`, or `both`) based on where the fix lives. Framework findings (agent prompts, hooks, pipeline logic in `plugins/autonomous-dev/`) are filed with `gh issue create -R akaszubski/autonomous-dev`; consumer findings omit the `-R` flag; `both` findings produce two cross-referenced issues. Routing heuristics and a routing table with five finding-type categories are embedded in the agent prompt. Batch mode includes `target_repo` annotations in its output for informational purposes without filing issues. `docs/WORKFLOW-DISCIPLINE.md` gains a "Cross-Repo Finding Routing" subsection covering the decision heuristic and examples. `docs/AGENTS.md` CIA entry updated to document the new routing capability. 5 acceptance tests in `tests/unit/test_cia_finding_routing.py`.

- **Regression test enforcement for bug-fix commits and features** (Issue #737): New `bugfix_detector.py` shared library provides three functions: `is_bugfix_feature(description, labels)` detects bug-fix intent from feature descriptions and GitHub labels; `is_bugfix_commit(message)` matches `fix:`, `bugfix:`, and `hotfix:` conventional-commit prefixes; `get_test_count(project_root)` counts `def test_*` functions across all `.py` files under `tests/`. New `enforce_regression_test.py` PreCommit hook blocks `fix:`, `bugfix:`, and `hotfix:` prefixed commits when no test files are staged; follows stick+carrot pattern with `REQUIRED NEXT ACTION` directive; fails open when library is unavailable. `implement.md` gains a `BASELINE_TEST_COUNT` capture at STEP 1 (before any changes) and a `HARD GATE: Regression Test Requirement` section at STEP 8 that blocks bug-fix features when `CURRENT_TEST_COUNT <= BASELINE_TEST_COUNT`. Hook registered in `install_manifest.json`, `settings.local.json`, and `settings.autonomous-dev.json`. 3 test files: `tests/unit/lib/test_bugfix_detector.py`, `tests/unit/hooks/test_enforce_regression_test.py`, `tests/unit/test_acceptance_regression_test_enforcement.py`. `docs/HOOKS.md`, `docs/HOOK-REGISTRY.md`, `docs/LIBRARIES.md`, and `docs/ARCHITECTURE-OVERVIEW.md` updated.

- **Adaptive replanning for implementer blocking signals** (Issue #730): New `blocking_signal_classifier.py` library provides three-tier classification of tool-execution errors into RECOVERABLE (ModuleNotFoundError, FileNotFoundError, ImportError, AttributeError, command not found), STRUCTURAL (SyntaxError, IndentationError, TabError), and NOT_BLOCKING. `classify_blocking_signal(error_output)` returns a `BlockingSignal` dataclass with `signal_type`, `error_name`, `error_detail`, and `suggested_action`. `format_mini_replan_directive(signal, *, cycle)` formats a structured `[MINI-REPLAN cycle N/2]` directive for the implementer. `sanitize_error_for_directive()` truncates and strips newlines to prevent log injection. Implementer agent gains a new "HARD GATE: Mini-Replan on Blocking Signals" section requiring a structured corrective-action cycle (identify → determine action → apply → re-run → evaluate) instead of blind retries, with escalation after 2 failed cycles. Mini-replan cycles are separate from the existing retry budget (Issue #708). 22 unit tests in `tests/unit/lib/test_blocking_signal_classifier.py`. `docs/AGENTS.md` implementer entry updated. `docs/LIBRARIES.md` gains entry 86. `docs/ARCHITECTURE-OVERVIEW.md` library count updated to 189.

- **Inline truncation warnings and context budget monitoring** (Issue #729): New `context_budget_monitor.py` library provides two capabilities. `truncate_output(text, *, max_chars=12000, tail_chars=500)` truncates oversized outputs and inserts an inline `[TRUNCATED: N chars removed. Showing first K + last T chars]` marker at the cut point so downstream agents know content was removed — both head+tail and head-only paths supported. `check_context_budget(current_tokens, max_tokens, *, warn_threshold=0.80, critical_threshold=0.95)` returns `None` below 80%, an advisory note between 80-95%, and a critical warning at 95%+. `estimate_tokens(text)` provides a rough word-count-based token estimate. `implement.md` STEP 10b VERBATIM PASSING updated to require coordinators include the `[TRUNCATED: N chars removed]` marker when truncating diffs passed to security-auditor. `docs/LIBRARIES.md` gains entry 85. `docs/ARCHITECTURE-OVERVIEW.md` library count updated to 188.

Reviewer performs a two-tier programmatic check for each entry before issuing any verdict — Tier 1 (file exists, file non-empty) is blocking; Tier 2 (verification signal matches via Read/Grep) is blocking. Missing or empty manifest triggers REQUEST_CHANGES in full pipeline mode. `implement.md` VERBATIM PASSING section updated to instruct coordinators to preserve the Evidence Manifest when truncating long implementer output. `docs/AGENTS.md` implementer and reviewer entries updated to document these requirements.

The shrinkage gate fires whenever a baseline exists, with no pipeline-active requirement; the first invocation seeds the baseline via `record_prompt_baseline` using `issue_number=0` as a hook sentinel. The entire baseline block is wrapped in a try/except to fail open on any error. 10 regression tests in `tests/unit/hooks/test_validate_prompt_integrity_shrinkage.py` cover non-critical bypass, floor-before-baseline ordering, seeding, threshold boundaries, fail-open behavior, actionable block messages, and 25% parameter verification.

### Changed
- **Implementer prompt templates expanded to enforce no-stubs and test-alongside-code** (Issue #726): `implement-batch.md` example implementer invocation template expanded from ~40 words to 95+ words by adding explicit requirements for working code (no stubs, no `NotImplementedError`, no pass placeholders), test-driven development (write unit tests alongside implementation, not after), and a structured output format. `implement-fix.md` STEP F3 implementer prompt template expanded from ~75 words to 95+ words by adding a regression test step, no-stubs enforcement clause, output format requirement, and self-validating word count instruction. 8 regression tests added in `tests/unit/commands/test_implementer_prompt_minimum.py` covering minimum word counts and required clause presence for both batch and fix mode templates.

### Changed
- **Prompt integrity 80-word minimum extended to doc-master, implementer, and planner** (Issue #725): `implement-batch.md` STEP B3 HARD GATE previously required only security-auditor and reviewer to receive at least 80 words in batch prompts. The gate now covers doc-master, implementer, and planner as well, matching the `COMPRESSION_CRITICAL_AGENTS` set in `prompt_integrity.py`. Structured 80+ word prompt templates added at three doc-master invocation points in `implement.md` (STEP 10 parallel, STEP 10c sequential, STEP L4 light mode). 14 regression tests added in `tests/unit/commands/test_batch_doc_master_prompt_minimum.py`.

### Fixed
- **Security-auditor prompt word count enforcement fires regardless of pipeline state** (Issue #716): `validate_prompt_integrity()` in `unified_pre_tool.py` previously returned early when `_is_pipeline_active()` was false, allowing below-minimum prompts to reach critical agents outside batch pipeline runs. Restructured to evaluate agent type and enforce the 80-word minimum before the pipeline-active check; baseline shrinkage detection (>25% compression vs first-issue baseline) remains pipeline-scoped. `implement-fix.md` STEP F1 now initializes the pipeline state file so that hook enforcement is active during fix mode; STEP F4 adds a FORBIDDEN rule against sending critical agents under-minimum prompts. Three regression tests added to `tests/unit/hooks/test_prompt_integrity_enforcement.py`.

### Added
- **Cluster selector for drain-queue tracker filtering** (Issue #1284): New pure function `select_next_cluster(clusters)` in `plugins/autonomous-dev/lib/cluster_selector.py` filters tracker-shaped issues from drain-queue candidates and prefers their lowest-numbered open leaf children when present. Detects trackers via: title prefix `[TRACKER]`, label combination "root-cause" + "enhancement", or body structure with ≥2 phase headings + ≥2 issue references. When encountering a tracker with open leaves, returns a synthetic single-issue cluster pointing to the lowest leaf. Not yet wired into `/drain-queue` command — integration tracked separately. Added 5 unit tests in `tests/unit/lib/test_cluster_selector.py`.

- **Coordinator bypass verdict tracking** (Issue #1279): Extended `record_plan_critic_skipped()` with optional `bypass_reason` parameter and added new `write_coordinator_bypass_verdict()` function in `pipeline_completion_state.py` to enable drain-queue coordinators to record machine-readable audit trails when bypassing plan-critic for mechanical extensions. New `/drain-queue` command documentation section "Coordinator Bypass of plan-critic" details the two-call protocol. 3 new tests in `tests/regression/test_issue_1279_coordinator_bypass_verdict.py` verify the bypass tracking logic.
- **Active security scanning in security-auditor** (Issue #710): New `active_security_scanner.py` library provides three active scan modes invoked by the security-auditor agent at STEP 0 before passive review. `dependency_audit(project_root)` parses `requirements.txt`/`pyproject.toml` and checks against a built-in advisory database (CVEs for django, flask, requests, urllib3, cryptography, pillow, jinja2, pyyaml), then runs `pip-audit` when available for comprehensive coverage. `credential_history_scan(project_root, *, max_commits=1000)` scans `git log --all -p` added lines against credential patterns, returning CRITICAL findings for any leaked secrets. `owasp_pattern_scan(file_paths)` checks Python source files against OWASP Top 10 patterns (A03 command/code/SQL injection, A05 debug misconfiguration, A10 SSRF), skipping test files and comments. `full_scan(project_root, changed_files=None)` orchestrates all three and returns an `ActiveScanReport` with findings sorted by `Severity` (CRITICAL→HIGH→MEDIUM→LOW), `scan_duration`, and `scans_completed`. `format_report(report)` renders Markdown output. New `secret_patterns.py` extracts credential regexes and OWASP patterns into a shared module consumed by both `active_security_scanner.py` and `hooks/security_scan.py` (single source of truth). CRITICAL or HIGH findings from active scan automatically fail the security audit. `docs/LIBRARIES.md` gains entries 83 and 84 for these libraries. `docs/ARCHITECTURE-OVERVIEW.md` security library list updated.

- **Cluster selector for drain-queue tracker filtering** (Issue #1284): New pure function `select_next_cluster(clusters)` in `plugins/autonomous-dev/lib/cluster_selector.py` filters tracker-shaped issues from drain-queue candidates and prefers their lowest-numbered open leaf children when present. Detects trackers via: title prefix `[TRACKER]`, label combination "root-cause" + "enhancement", or body structure with ≥2 phase headings + ≥2 issue references. When encountering a tracker with open leaves, returns a synthetic single-issue cluster pointing to the lowest leaf. Not yet wired into `/drain-queue` command — integration tracked separately. Added 5 unit tests in `tests/unit/lib/test_cluster_selector.py`.

New `/drain-queue` command documentation section "Coordinator Bypass of plan-critic" details the two-call protocol. 3 new tests in `tests/regression/test_issue_1279_coordinator_bypass_verdict.py` verify the bypass tracking logic.
- **Pipeline efficiency analyzer** (Issue #714): New `pipeline_efficiency_analyzer.py` library provides data-driven agent optimization recommendations after 5+ pipeline runs. `analyze_efficiency(observations)` groups historical timing entries by agent type and runs three analyses: `detect_model_tier_recommendations()` flags stable agents (quality CV < 0.3) with efficient token usage (median tokens/word < 100) as model downgrade candidates and warns on prompt bloat (tokens/word > 500); `detect_token_trends()` uses linear regression to detect rising token usage over sequential runs (flagged when slope > 0 and R² > 0.5); IQR outlier detection surfaces anomalous token usage spikes. Circuit breaker caps findings at 5 per report. `format_efficiency_report()` renders Markdown output. `pipeline_timing_analyzer.save_timing_entry()` extended to persist `total_tokens` and `tool_uses` fields for cross-run trend analysis. `continuous-improvement-analyst` gains check #14 (Pipeline Efficiency Analysis) at Step 5.5 of Full Mode — advisory only, never issues. `docs/LIBRARIES.md` gains entry 82 for this library.

- **Per-step time budgets for pipeline agents** (Issue #705): `pipeline_timing_analyzer.py` gains three budget functions: `load_time_budgets(config_path)` reads per-agent budgets from `plugins/autonomous-dev/config/pipeline_time_budgets.json` (falls back to `STATIC_THRESHOLDS` when file is missing or malformed); `check_budget_violation(agent_type, duration_seconds, budgets)` returns `None` when within budget or a violation dict with `level` ("warning" at ≥80% of budget, "exceeded" at >100%); `format_budget_warning(violation)` formats a stick+carrot message with a `REQUIRED NEXT ACTION` directive pointing to the config file. `session_activity_logger.py` hooks into agent completions to log `BudgetWarning` entries when violations are detected. `continuous-improvement-analyst` check #11 extended to scan activity logs for `BudgetWarning` entries and escalate agents with 3+ consecutive `exceeded` violations to a `[BUDGET-EXCEEDED]` GitHub issue. New config file `plugins/autonomous-dev/config/pipeline_time_budgets.json` sets budgets for all 8 pipeline agents. `docs/LIBRARIES.md` updated for `pipeline_timing_analyzer.py`.

New `/drain-queue` command documentation section "Coordinator Bypass of plan-critic" details the two-call protocol. 3 new tests in `tests/regression/test_issue_1279_coordinator_bypass_verdict.py` verify the bypass tracking logic.
- **Implementer output self-validation gate** (Issue #707): After tests pass, implementer MUST run semantic validation — smoke test with realistic input, output format check, boundary check, and error path check — before declaring completion. FORBIDDEN: accepting empty/placeholder/nonsensical outputs. Added to `agents/implementer.md`.
- **Error recovery with retry budgets and forced pivots** (Issue #708): Implementer gets max 2 retries per approach. Same error twice forces pivot to alternative strategy (simplify → alternative library → decompose → escalate). Error log tracks what was tried and why. Added to `agents/implementer.md`.
- **Pre-execution tool documentation research** (Issue #706): Implementer MUST read `--help` output before using unfamiliar CLI tools. Known-safe tool list exempts standard tools (git, python, pytest, etc.). Researcher agent gains tool documentation gathering step. Added to `agents/implementer.md` and `agents/researcher.md`.
- **Test count regression gate to prevent behavioral test deletion gaming** (Issue #711): `coverage_baseline.check_test_count_regression()` blocks the STEP 8 quality gate when the test count drops by more than `min(10%, 20 tests)` from baseline, detecting cases where behavioral tests are silently removed and replaced with fewer structural absence-checks. `step5_quality_gate.run_quality_gate()` now wires this check as a fourth required condition (alongside test pass/fail, coverage regression, and skip regression), surfacing a `test_count_regression` key in the result dict and including the message in the gate summary. `agents/reviewer.md` gains a "HARD GATE: Test Deletion Detection" section that requires the reviewer to flag deleted test files with >50% line reduction, deleted issue-traced regression tests, and structural absence-check replacements for behavioral tests. `agents/security-auditor.md` gains a "HARD GATE: Security Test Integrity Check" section that flags deletion or weakening of security-related tests (covering auth, injection, XSS, CSRF, SSRF, secrets, permissions) as HIGH severity, even when structural-only replacement tests are present. `docs/AGENTS.md` and `docs/LIBRARIES.md` updated to document the new checks.

### Changed
- **Silent exception swallowing escalated from WARNING to BLOCKING in reviewer** (Issue #715): `plugins/autonomous-dev/agents/reviewer.md` observability check now classifies four silent exception patterns as BLOCKING findings that trigger the STEP 6.5 Remediation Gate: bare `except: pass`, `except Exception` without `raise` or `exc_info=True`, `contextlib.suppress()` wrapping non-trivial operations, and `finally` blocks with `return`/`break`/`continue` suppressing pending exceptions. Previously these were advisory WARNING findings. `plugins/autonomous-dev/config/known_bypass_patterns.json` adds a `silent_exception_swallowing` pattern entry (issue #715, severity critical) for detection by continuous-improvement-analyst. `plugins/autonomous-dev/skills/observability/SKILL.md` Hard Rules section expanded with matching FORBIDDEN and REQUIRED tiers. `docs/AGENTS.md` reviewer skills corrected to reflect actual frontmatter (`python-standards, code-review, security-patterns, refactoring-patterns`). `docs/ARCHITECTURE-OVERVIEW.md` reviewer description updated to reflect current four-category check structure.

- **`covers_index.py` library for doc-master optimization** (Issue #713): New `covers_index.py` library pre-computes the source-path to doc-file mapping that doc-master previously rebuilt on every invocation by scanning all 23+ `docs/*.md` files for `covers:` YAML frontmatter. `build_covers_index(docs_dir)` scans once and returns a dict; `get_affected_docs(changed_files, index)` queries it using exact, prefix (`/`), and glob (`*`) matching. `save_covers_index()` and `load_covers_index()` handle JSON persistence with metadata keys. `scripts/build_covers_index.py` is the CLI to regenerate `docs/covers_index.json`. 24 unit tests in `tests/unit/lib/test_covers_index.py`. `docs/LIBRARIES.md` gains entry 81 for this library.

### Fixed
- **Test mock signatures updated to accept `pipeline_mode` keyword argument**: Six `mock_check` functions in `tests/unit/hooks/test_native_tool_auto_approval.py` (`TestSubagentTypePriority`) were missing the `pipeline_mode=None` parameter added to `check_ordering_prerequisites` in `unified_pre_tool.py`. The mocks raised `TypeError` when called with the new keyword argument, causing 7 test failures. Added `pipeline_mode=None` to all six mock signatures to restore test compatibility.

- **`DUPLICATE_COVERAGE` detection precision improved in `test_pruning_analyzer.py`** (Issue #701): Duplicate detection changed from per-call matching (flagging any two tests that call the same function with identical args) to per-test subset granularity (flagging a test only when its entire non-framework call signature set is a strict subset of another test's). Expanded skip list excludes test framework utilities (`Mock`, `patch`, `assert_called_*`, `assertEqual`, `pytest.raises`, etc.) from signature extraction, eliminating false positives caused by shared setup boilerplate. 4 regression tests added in `tests/unit/lib/test_test_pruning_analyzer.py`. `docs/LIBRARIES.md` detection-category table updated to reflect the new subset-based behavior.

- **continuous-improvement-analyst check #11 call instruction corrected**: `agents/continuous-improvement-analyst.md` line 84 previously omitted the required arguments to `format_timing_report()`. Updated to `format_timing_report(timings, analysis.get('findings', []))` to match the function signature in `pipeline_timing_analyzer.py`, which requires `(timings: list[AgentTiming], findings: list[TimingFinding])`.

- **`/autoresearch` command — autonomous experiment loop** (Issue #654): New `/autoresearch` command runs a hypothesis-test-measure loop on a single target file (restricted to `agents/*.md` and `skills/*/SKILL.md`). Each iteration: hypothesize a change, apply it, run a user-supplied benchmark script that emits `METRIC: <float>`, commit if metric improved by at least `--min-improvement` (default 0.01), or revert on failure. Stall detection halts the loop after `--max-stall` consecutive failures (default 3). All experiments run on a dedicated `autoresearch/<target>-<timestamp>` branch. New `autoresearch_engine.py` library provides `ExperimentConfig`, `ExperimentHistory` (append-only JSONL log), `validate_target()`, `validate_metric()`, `run_metric()`, `commit_improvement()`, `revert_target()`, and `check_stall()`. `docs/ARCHITECTURE-OVERVIEW.md` command count updated from 21 to 22. `docs/LIBRARIES.md` gains entry for `autoresearch_engine.py`. `README.md` component table updated to reflect 22 commands.

### Fixed
- **docs/AGENTS.md model tier table corrected**: Agent model tier assignments were stale — `issue-creator` was listed under Tier 2 (Sonnet) but uses `haiku`; `planner` and `implementer` were listed under Tier 2 (Sonnet) but use `opus`; `security-auditor` was listed under Tier 3 (Opus) but uses `sonnet`; tier agent counts were wrong (Tier 1 header said 2, should be 3; Tier 2 said 9, should be 8). Corrected all assignments and counts to match actual agent frontmatter. `docs/ARCHITECTURE-OVERVIEW.md` was already accurate.

- **mobile-tester agent for iOS/Android E2E testing** (Issue #657): New `mobile-tester` specialist agent runs at STEP 9.8 of the `/implement` pipeline, invoked only when changed files include mobile patterns (`*.swift`, `*.kt`, `*.dart`, `ios/`, `android/`, `Podfile`, `build.gradle`, `pubspec.yaml`) and Appium MCP or Maestro CLI is available. Implements a three-layer testing stack: Appium MCP for interactive real-time element interaction, Maestro CLI for persistent YAML regression flows in `.maestro/`, and xcodebuild/Gradle/Flutter build verification for native source changes. Hard gates enforce simulator-only execution (no physical production devices), screenshot isolation to designated directories, and 60-second per-test-case timeouts using element wait conditions only. Verdict is advisory (`MOBILE-TESTER-VERDICT: PASS` or `SKIP`) — never blocks the pipeline. `docs/AGENTS.md` and `docs/ARCHITECTURE-OVERVIEW.md` updated to reflect 14 active agents and the STEP 9.8 workflow addition.

### Fixed
- **docs: COMPRESSION_CRITICAL_AGENTS corrected in LIBRARIES.md** (Issue #696): `docs/LIBRARIES.md` description of `prompt_integrity.py` listed only `{"security-auditor", "reviewer"}` as compression-critical agents. The actual constant in both `prompt_integrity.py` and `pipeline_intent_validator.py` now includes `"researcher-local"`, `"researcher"`, `"implementer"`, `"planner"`, and `"doc-master"` — expanded progressively via Issues #666 and #696. Documentation updated to reflect the full 7-agent set.

### Fixed
- **Coverage threshold suppressed for partial test runs** (Issue #699): Running a single test file or test node (e.g. `pytest tests/unit/hooks/test_foo.py`) previously exited non-zero because the global `--cov-fail-under` threshold from `pytest.ini` was unreachable when only a fraction of source was exercised. `tests/conftest.py` gains `_is_partial_test_run()`, which detects specific `.py` file arguments, node-id (`::`) arguments, and `-k`/`-m` filter flags, and a `pytest_configure` hook (registered `trylast` so pytest-cov is already loaded) that sets `cov_fail_under` to 0 on both `config.option` and the pytest-cov plugin's own options namespace. Full suite runs (`pytest` or `pytest tests/`) are unaffected and continue to enforce the CI threshold. Regression tests added in `tests/regression/test_coverage_threshold_isolation.py`.

- **Python inline bypass detection expanded to cover rename/replace operations** (Issue #698): `python_write_detector.py` gains detection of `os.rename(src, dst)`, `os.replace(src, dst)`, `Path("src").rename("dst")`, and `Path("src").replace("dst")` as write-equivalent operations. Both AST and regex paths are updated; aliased `os` module (`import os as o`) and direct-import style (`from os import rename`) are tracked via the AST visitor. `unified_pre_tool.py` inline regex fallback in `_extract_bash_file_writes` and `_check_bash_infra_writes` is updated with matching patterns. Previously these patterns evaded the Bash-wrapped infrastructure write guard. New tests in `tests/unit/lib/test_python_write_detector.py` and regression tests in `tests/unit/hooks/test_bash_write_bypass.py` / `tests/regression/test_bash_write_bypass_regression.py`.

- **Mode-aware prerequisite filtering in `agent_ordering_gate.py`** (Issue #697): `check_ordering_prerequisites()` gains a `pipeline_mode` parameter (`"full"`, `"light"`, `"fix"`, `"tdd-first"`) that skips ordering prerequisites for agents not required by the current mode. Previously, `--fix` mode pipelines (implementer, reviewer, doc-master) were blocked because the `planner → implementer` prerequisite fired even though planner is not part of the fix pipeline. `unified_pre_tool.py` gains `_get_pipeline_mode_from_state()`, which reads the `mode` field from the pipeline state file and passes it to `check_ordering_prerequisites()`, defaulting to `"full"` when the state file is absent. The `FIX_PIPELINE_AGENTS` constant documents the agents belonging to fix mode. 8 regression tests added in `tests/unit/lib/test_agent_ordering_gate.py` via `TestPipelineModeFiltering`.

### Fixed
- **STEP 6 silent skip eliminated — explicit skip/execute logging required** (Issue #646): `implement.md` STEP 6 (acceptance test generation) previously had no required output format, allowing the coordinator to silently transition from STEP 5 to STEP 8 without generating acceptance tests or explaining why. Added a required `STEP 6: SKIPPED (reason)` / `STEP 6: EXECUTED (N tests)` log format, a banner-on-skip requirement so the step is always visible in the audit trail, and a FORBIDDEN list explicitly prohibiting silent skips, jumps from STEP 5 to STEP 8 without passing through STEP 6, and skipping when `tests/genai/conftest.py` exists and mode is not `--tdd-first`. Nine regression tests added in `tests/regression/test_step6_silent_skip_fix.py` covering skip format, FORBIDDEN list content, and banner requirement.

When encountering a tracker with open leaves, returns a synthetic single-issue cluster pointing to the lowest leaf. Not yet wired into `/drain-queue` command — integration tracked separately.
