- **Coordinator bypass verdict tracking** (Issue #1279): Extended `record_plan_critic_skipped()` with optional `bypass_reason` parameter and added new `write_coordinator_bypass_verdict()` function in `pipeline_completion_state.py` to enable drain-queue coordinators to record machine-readable audit trails when bypassing plan-critic for mechanical extensions. New `/drain-queue` command documentation section "Coordinator Bypass of plan-critic" details the two-call protocol. 3 new tests in `tests/regression/test_issue_1279_coordinator_bypass_verdict.py` verify the bypass tracking logic.
- **Path Depth Verification HARD GATE in implementer agent** (Issue #694): `plugins/autonomous-dev/agents/implementer.md` gains a new "HARD GATE: Path Depth Verification" section requiring the implementer to count directory levels before using `Path(__file__).resolve().parents[N]` in test files. The section includes a verification method with concrete examples for common test paths (`tests/test_foo.py` → `parents[1]`, `tests/unit/test_foo.py` → `parents[2]`, etc.) and forbids placing test files in double-nested directories. Five regression tests added in `tests/unit/agents/test_pipeline_agent_quality_audit.py` covering the section heading, verification examples, forbidden-without-counting behavior, and double-nested directory prohibition.

### Fixed
- **Reviewer ghost fix — minimum file read requirement** (Issue #659): `plugins/autonomous-dev/agents/reviewer.md` gains a new HARD GATE section requiring the reviewer to use the Read tool on each changed file before issuing any verdict. Minimum tool use thresholds enforced by implementation size (1-50 lines: 2 uses; 51-200 lines: 3 uses; 200+ lines: 5 uses). Ghost reviews — issuing any verdict with 0 tool uses by reviewing only from prompt context — are now explicitly FORBIDDEN. Five regression tests added in `tests/unit/agents/test_pipeline_agent_quality_audit.py` covering the section heading, REQUIRED TOOL ACTIONS language, threshold definitions, and the main FORBIDDEN list entry. `docs/AGENTS.md` updated: reviewer model corrected from Haiku (Tier 1) to Sonnet (Tier 2), and the Minimum File Read Requirement enforcement documented alongside existing read-only enforcement.

### Fixed
- **Expand compression-critical agent coverage to include researcher agents** (Issue #666): `COMPRESSION_CRITICAL_AGENTS` in `prompt_integrity.py` and the mirrored constant in `pipeline_intent_validator.py` expanded from `{"security-auditor", "reviewer"}` to include `"researcher-local"` and `"researcher"`. Researcher agents are also subject to context compression in long batch runs, and their prompts must meet the 80-word minimum to prevent silent degradation of research quality. Tests in `tests/unit/lib/test_prompt_integrity.py` and `tests/unit/lib/test_prompt_compression_detection.py` updated to cover the expanded set.

### Added
- **Cluster selector for drain-queue tracker filtering** (Issue #1284): New pure function `select_next_cluster(clusters)` in `plugins/autonomous-dev/lib/cluster_selector.py` filters tracker-shaped issues from drain-queue candidates and prefers their lowest-numbered open leaf children when present. Detects trackers via: title prefix `[TRACKER]`, label combination "root-cause" + "enhancement", or body structure with ≥2 phase headings + ≥2 issue references. When encountering a tracker with open leaves, returns a synthetic single-issue cluster pointing to the lowest leaf. Not yet wired into `/drain-queue` command — integration tracked separately. Added 5 unit tests in `tests/unit/lib/test_cluster_selector.py`.

- **Coordinator bypass verdict tracking** (Issue #1279): Extended `record_plan_critic_skipped()` with optional `bypass_reason` parameter and added new `write_coordinator_bypass_verdict()` function in `pipeline_completion_state.py` to enable drain-queue coordinators to record machine-readable audit trails when bypassing plan-critic for mechanical extensions. New `/drain-queue` command documentation section "Coordinator Bypass of plan-critic" details the two-call protocol. 3 new tests in `tests/regression/test_issue_1279_coordinator_bypass_verdict.py` verify the bypass tracking logic.
- **Researcher-skip bypass pattern detection** (Issue #671): New `research_skip_entire_batch` pattern added to `known_bypass_patterns.json` (both `.claude/config/` and `plugins/autonomous-dev/config/`). Detects when all issues in a batch skip both `researcher-local` and `researcher` agents by over-applying the pre-researched content exemption. Fires as WARNING (not CRITICAL) when more than 50% of batch issues skip research; individual per-issue skips with valid `/create-issue` cache remain valid. Regression tests added in `tests/regression/progression/test_issue_671_research_skip_bypass.py`.

### Fixed
- **doc-master verdict read race condition** (Issue #682): The coordinator would attempt to read the doc-master verdict by grepping transcript files before they had fully flushed to disk, causing false `[DOC-VERDICT-MISSING]` warnings even when the verdict was emitted. Fixed by adding explicit guidance to both `implement.md` STEP 12 and `implement-batch.md` STEP B3: use the Agent tool's return value directly instead of grepping transcript files, and wait 3 seconds for filesystem flush only when transcript parsing is unavoidable. The retry path now also uses a reduced-context re-invocation (current file list + feature description only) rather than full context. 14 regression tests added in `tests/regression/test_issue_682_doc_verdict_race.py` verifying that the mitigation language is present in both command files.

### Fixed
- **Per-issue CIA batch mode reporting from context alone without tool use** (Issue #683): Per-issue `continuous-improvement-analyst` agents in batch mode completed in under 13 seconds with 0 tool uses because the coordinator pre-digested all context in the prompt, making actual file inspection unnecessary. Added a `REQUIRED TOOL ACTIONS` block to the per-issue CIA prompt template in `implement-batch.md` that forces the agent to run `git diff HEAD -- tests/` (checking for skip markers and weakened assertions), read `.claude/config/known_bypass_patterns.json` (verifying no undocumented bypasses), and grep for `pytest.mark.skip` count. The description line also uses "3-5 REQUIRED tool calls" to make the mandatory nature explicit. Regression tests added in `tests/regression/test_cia_batch_prompt_tool_actions.py`.

### Fixed
- **Expanded doc-master prompt template in `--fix` mode STEP F4** (Issue #693): `implement-fix.md` STEP F4 doc-master prompt template was ~20 words of template text, causing 6/9 invocations to produce under-100-word verdicts. Template expanded to >= 80 words with REQUIRED STEPS (SCAN, SEMANTIC COMPARISON, DOC-DRIFT-VERDICT) enforcing the same three-step process used by the standalone doc-master agent. Verdict options (DOCS-UPDATED, NO-UPDATE-NEEDED, DOCS-DRIFT-FOUND) are now explicit in the prompt. Regression tests added in `tests/unit/commands/test_fix_mode_prompt_integrity.py` covering doc-master template word count, DOC-DRIFT-VERDICT requirement, SCAN step presence, and SEMANTIC COMPARISON step presence.
- **Minimum prompt word count violation in `--fix` mode STEP F4** (Issue #687): `implement-fix.md` STEP F4 reviewer and security-auditor prompt templates were too short (~20 words of template text), causing the constructed prompt to fall below the 80-word minimum when the coordinator summarized implementer output. Templates expanded to include explicit review checklists (reviewer) and security audit checklists (security-auditor), each meeting the >= 80 word threshold independently of dynamic content. Added a Prompt Integrity Validation hard gate requiring the coordinator to call `validate_prompt_word_count()` from `prompt_integrity.py` before invoking each critical agent, and a Verbatim Implementer Output hard gate forbidding summarization or condensing of implementer output before passing it to reviewer or security-auditor. Regression tests added in `tests/unit/commands/test_fix_mode_prompt_integrity.py` covering template word counts, verbatim requirement documentation, and the FORBIDDEN list.

### Fixed
- **`.worktrees` exclusion in test analyzers** (Issue #691): `TestLifecycleManager._collect_test_paths()` now skips test files whose path contains a `.worktrees` component, preventing analysis of tests from other in-progress worktrees. `TestPruningAnalyzer.SKIP_DIRS` gained `.worktrees` so `_discover_test_files()` applies the same exclusion. Regression tests added in `tests/unit/lib/test_worktree_exclusion.py`. `docs/LIBRARIES.md` skip-directory list for `test_pruning_analyzer.py` updated to include `.worktrees`.

### Added
- **Cluster selector for drain-queue tracker filtering** (Issue #1284): New pure function `select_next_cluster(clusters)` in `plugins/autonomous-dev/lib/cluster_selector.py` filters tracker-shaped issues from drain-queue candidates and prefers their lowest-numbered open leaf children when present. Detects trackers via: title prefix `[TRACKER]`, label combination "root-cause" + "enhancement", or body structure with ≥2 phase headings + ≥2 issue references. When encountering a tracker with open leaves, returns a synthetic single-issue cluster pointing to the lowest leaf. Not yet wired into `/drain-queue` command — integration tracked separately. Added 5 unit tests in `tests/unit/lib/test_cluster_selector.py`.

- **Coordinator bypass verdict tracking** (Issue #1279): Extended `record_plan_critic_skipped()` with optional `bypass_reason` parameter and added new `write_coordinator_bypass_verdict()` function in `pipeline_completion_state.py` to enable drain-queue coordinators to record machine-readable audit trails when bypassing plan-critic for mechanical extensions. New `/drain-queue` command documentation section "Coordinator Bypass of plan-critic" details the two-call protocol. 3 new tests in `tests/regression/test_issue_1279_coordinator_bypass_verdict.py` verify the bypass tracking logic.
- **Agent launch tracking for parallel-mode ordering defense-in-depth** (Issue #686): `pipeline_completion_state.py` gains two new functions — `record_agent_launch(session_id, agent_type, *, issue_number=0)` records that an agent was launched (called from `unified_pre_tool.py` in PreToolUse before the agent runs) and `get_launched_agents(session_id, *, issue_number=0)` returns the set of launched agents. `unified_pre_tool.py` now calls `record_agent_launch()` before reading completed agents, then passes `launched_agents` to `check_ordering_prerequisites()`. This enables the parallel-mode guard (added in #669) to distinguish "prerequisite running concurrently" from "prerequisite never launched", closing the bypass where `security-auditor` could run in parallel mode even when `reviewer` had never started. State file gains a `"launches"` key alongside `"completions"`.

### Fixed
- **Stale hook test baseline in CIA agent definition** (Issue #692): `continuous-improvement-analyst.md` referenced an outdated hook failure baseline of "unified_pre_tool: 6 = 14 total". After native tool auto-approval test fixes (Issue #690), the unified_pre_tool failures were resolved and only `batch_permission_approver: 8` remain as known pre-existing failures. Updated the baseline to "batch_permission_approver: 8 = 8 total" so the CIA agent's hook regression check no longer fires false positives. Regression test `tests/unit/test_ci_analyst_pipeline_detection.py` added to prevent future baseline staleness.
- **Test alignment for mtime-based staleness check in infrastructure protection** (Issue #685): Updated `tests/unit/hooks/test_infrastructure_protection.py` to match the mtime-based file staleness detection logic in `unified_pre_tool.py`. No behavior change to production code; pure test fix to eliminate false failures caused by the test asserting against the old inode/hash-based staleness approach.
- **Parallel mode ordering bypass in `agent_ordering_gate.py`** (Issue #669): Added `launched_agents` parameter to `check_ordering_prerequisites()` so that parallel mode enforcement distinguishes between "running concurrently" and "skipped entirely". Previously, parallel mode fully relaxed the `reviewer → security-auditor` constraint, allowing `security-auditor` to run even when `reviewer` had never been launched. Now, if the prerequisite has not been launched at all the check blocks; if it has been launched but not yet completed the check passes with a `warning` field on `GateResult`. `unified_pre_tool.py` logs the warning via the `unified_pre_tool.ordering` logger. Fail-open exceptions in the ordering check also now log a warning noting that the ordering guarantee is not enforced, improving observability for security-critical cases. 14 regression tests added covering launched-vs-completed distinctions and warning propagation.

### Fixed
- **False positive ordering and context-dropping findings in mixed-mode batches** (Issues #680, #681): `validate_step_ordering()` in `pipeline_intent_validator.py` now groups agent events by `batch_issue_number` when batch events are present, checking ordering independently within each issue group. This prevents false CRITICAL `step_ordering` findings when `--fix` issues (implementer-only, no planner/researcher) run alongside full-pipeline issues in the same batch session. `detect_context_dropping()` now skips consecutive-pair comparisons across issue boundaries in batch sessions, preventing false positives from treating the final agent result of one issue as the expected context window for the first agent of the next issue.
- **Hook path resolution in git worktrees** (Issue #651): All 6 settings template files replaced `$(git rev-parse --show-toplevel)` with `$(dirname "$(git rev-parse --path-format=absolute --git-common-dir)")` in hook command paths. `--show-toplevel` returns the worktree directory when invoked inside a git worktree, causing hook invocations to resolve to the wrong path; `--git-common-dir` always points to the shared `.git` directory of the main repository regardless of the active worktree. Regression test added: `tests/regression/regression/test_issue_651_worktree_hook_paths.py`. Existing `tests/regression/regression/test_issue_513_hook_paths_subdirectory.py` updated to cover the new resolution strategy.

Detects trackers via: title prefix `[TRACKER]`, label combination "root-cause" + "enhancement", or body structure with ≥2 phase headings + ≥2 issue references. When encountering a tracker with open leaves, returns a synthetic single-issue cluster pointing to the lowest leaf. Not yet wired into `/drain-queue` command — integration tracked separately. New `/drain-queue` command documentation section "Coordinator Bypass of plan-critic" details the two-call protocol. 3 new tests in `tests/regression/test_issue_1279_coordinator_bypass_verdict.py` verify the bypass tracking logic.
- **Test lifecycle management — pruning, intent alignment, acceptance-first hierarchy** (Issue #673): New `test_lifecycle_manager.py` composition library orchestrates four existing analyzers (TestIssueTracer, TestPruningAnalyzer, tier_registry, coverage_baseline) into a unified `TestHealthReport`. Each analyzer runs in an isolated error boundary so partial failures produce advisory output rather than crashes. `TestLifecycleManager.analyze()` returns tracing findings, pruning candidates, tier distribution, and coverage baseline in a single call. `format_dashboard(report)` renders a markdown health dashboard. `check_issue_tracing(project_root, issue_number)` is a pipeline convenience wrapper used by STEP 13. `/improve` gains STEP 2.7 (Test Health Report): runs `TestLifecycleManager` and passes the dashboard to the continuous-improvement-analyst. The `continuous-improvement-analyst` agent gains check #12 (Test Lifecycle Health): flags `>20 prunable candidates`, `>50% untraced tests`, zero T0 acceptance tests, or an imbalanced tier pyramid. `docs/LIBRARIES.md` updated with entry 79 (`test_lifecycle_manager.py`); Core Libraries count updated from 79 to 80. `docs/ARCHITECTURE-OVERVIEW.md` library count updated from 191 to 192 and `test_lifecycle_manager.py` added to the Infrastructure key libraries list.

- **Skill effectiveness harness integrated into `/implement` and `/improve`** (Issue #643): New `skill_change_detector.py` library provides `detect_skill_changes()` (extracts modified skill names from `skills/*/SKILL.md` paths), `get_eval_status()` (checks eval prompts and baselines), `format_skill_eval_report()` (PASS/WARNING/BLOCK verdicts — delta < -0.10 blocks), and `get_weak_skills()` (surfaces skills below delta/pass-rate/freshness thresholds). `/implement` gains STEP 11.5 (Skill Effectiveness Gate): runs conditionally when skill files are modified; missing eval prompts or missing `OPENROUTER_API_KEY` produce advisory warnings only and never block the pipeline. `/improve` gains STEP 2.5 (Skill Effectiveness Report): surfaces weak, low-quality, or stale skills from baselines to the continuous-improvement-analyst for action. `docs/LIBRARIES.md` updated with entry 79 (`skill_change_detector.py`). - **Mock stub alignment in `TestSubagentTypePriority`** (Issue #690): Updated mock signatures in `tests/unit/hooks/test_native_tool_auto_approval.py` to match the current calling conventions used by `unified_pre_tool.py`. No behavior change to production code; pure test fix to eliminate failures caused by outdated mock stubs.
- **Regression tests for `subagent_type` priority in ordering gate** (Issue #636): Added 6 regression tests to `tests/unit/hooks/test_native_tool_auto_approval.py` covering `subagent_type` priority ordering in the agent ordering gate — verifying that `subagent_type` takes precedence over inferred agent identity when both are present, that ordering violations are correctly detected across varied invocation contexts, and that the gate does not produce false positives when ordering is valid.

Skips sessions with no completed pipeline agents (not a real run) and sessions that appear to be batch runs (any event has `batch_issue_number > 0`). - **Shallow doc-master sweep detection** (Issue #672): New `detect_doc_verdict_shallow()` function and `MIN_DOC_SWEEP_WORDS = 100` constant in `pipeline_intent_validator.py` detect doc-master completions that pass the existing `MIN_DOC_VERDICT_WORDS = 30` threshold (so they evade the missing-verdict check) but produce fewer than 100 words — indicating the agent only updated CHANGELOG without performing a real semantic drift sweep. Emits a `WARNING` finding with `finding_type="doc_verdict_shallow"`. Uses the same `_correlate_invocation_completion()` pairing strategy as `detect_doc_verdict_missing`, with a fallback scan when correlation fails. Integrated into `validate_pipeline_intent()`.

### Fixed
- **False positive `doc_verdict_missing` findings when correlation fails** (Issue #650): `detect_doc_verdict_missing()` in `pipeline_intent_validator.py` now performs a fallback scan of the full event list when `_correlate_invocation_completion()` returns no completion match for a doc-master invocation. If any doc-master completion event with `success=True` and `result_word_count >= MIN_DOC_VERDICT_WORDS` exists, the unmatched invocation is skipped rather than flagged as missing. This prevents spurious CRITICAL findings caused by session grouping, timestamp parsing, or time-window boundary edge cases in the correlation algorithm.
- **`gh issue create` self-block when invoked via issue-creating slash commands** (#647, #663): `unified_pre_tool.py` now auto-writes the command context file (`/tmp/autonomous_dev_cmd_context.json`) in the `NATIVE_TOOLS` fast path when a `Skill` tool invocation for an issue-creating command is detected. Previously, the context file was never written before the subsequent Bash `gh issue create` call fired, causing the hook to incorrectly block commands like `/create-issue`, `/plan-to-issues`, `/improve`, `/refactor`, and `/retrospective`. Regression test added: `tests/unit/hooks/test_gh_issue_create_selfblock.py`.

When total > 0 but covered == 0, a WARNING is appended (advisory only, never blocks). `implement.md` STEP 6 now saves the criteria-to-test mapping via `save_criteria_registry()` after generating acceptance tests. `docs/LIBRARIES.md` updated with entry 78 (`acceptance_criteria_tracker.py`) and the `step5_quality_gate.py` entry updated to describe the acceptance coverage feature. `docs/ARCHITECTURE-OVERVIEW.md` library count updated from 189 to 190.

- **Test-to-issue traceability library and `/audit --test-tracing`** (#675): New `test_issue_tracer.py` library scans `tests/` for 7 issue-reference patterns (class names, function names, docstrings, comments, `GH-NNN` shorthand, `@pytest.mark.issue()` markers, and keyword phrases such as `Closes #NNN`). Results are cross-referenced against live GitHub issues via `gh` CLI, producing a `TracingReport` with three finding categories: `UNTESTED_ISSUE` (open issue with no test ref), `ORPHANED_PAIR` (test references a closed issue), and `UNTRACED_TEST` (test file with zero issue refs). All findings are advisory (`info` severity). `/audit` gains a `--test-tracing` flag that runs `TestIssueTracer.analyze()` and prints the markdown table. `/implement` STEP 13 now runs a non-blocking tracing check after pipeline finalization and displays results without blocking the pipeline. `docs/LIBRARIES.md` updated with entry 77 (Core Libraries count 76→77). `docs/ARCHITECTURE-OVERVIEW.md` library count updated from 188 to 189 and `test_issue_tracer.py` added to the Infrastructure key libraries. `skills/testing-guide/SKILL.md` updated with convention docs for the tracing patterns.

### Added
- **Cluster selector for drain-queue tracker filtering** (Issue #1284): New pure function `select_next_cluster(clusters)` in `plugins/autonomous-dev/lib/cluster_selector.py` filters tracker-shaped issues from drain-queue candidates and prefers their lowest-numbered open leaf children when present. Detects trackers via: title prefix `[TRACKER]`, label combination "root-cause" + "enhancement", or body structure with ≥2 phase headings + ≥2 issue references. When encountering a tracker with open leaves, returns a synthetic single-issue cluster pointing to the lowest leaf. Not yet wired into `/drain-queue` command — integration tracked separately. Added 5 unit tests in `tests/unit/lib/test_cluster_selector.py`.

- **Coordinator bypass verdict tracking** (Issue #1279): Extended `record_plan_critic_skipped()` with optional `bypass_reason` parameter and added new `write_coordinator_bypass_verdict()` function in `pipeline_completion_state.py` to enable drain-queue coordinators to record machine-readable audit trails when bypassing plan-critic for mechanical extensions. New `/drain-queue` command documentation section "Coordinator Bypass of plan-critic" details the two-call protocol. 3 new tests in `tests/regression/test_issue_1279_coordinator_bypass_verdict.py` verify the bypass tracking logic.
- **AST-based test pruning analyzer (`test_pruning_analyzer.py`) and `/sweep --tests`** (#674): New `test_pruning_analyzer.py` library detects 5 categories of test hygiene issues via AST analysis: dead imports (imports referencing modules not found in source), archived references (imports from `archived/` paths), zero-assertion tests (pass-only bodies or placeholder `assert True`), duplicate coverage (two test functions calling the same function with identical arguments), and stale regression markers (`TestIssueNNN` / `test_issue_NNN` naming patterns). Each finding includes file path, line number, severity (HIGH/MEDIUM/LOW), and a `prunable` flag derived from `tier_registry.is_prunable()` — T2/T3 tier tests are safe candidates for removal; T0/T1 are not. Output is always informational; no files are auto-deleted. `/sweep --tests` now invokes the analyzer and displays the markdown report. `docs/LIBRARIES.md` updated with entry 76. `docs/ARCHITECTURE-OVERVIEW.md` library count updated from 187 to 188 and `test_pruning_analyzer.py` added to the Infrastructure key libraries.

Maps test directories to tier IDs, lifecycle policies (`permanent` / `stable` / `semi-stable` / `ephemeral`), pytest markers, and max duration constraints. `tests/conftest.py` now imports `build_directory_markers()` from `tier_registry` at startup instead of maintaining a hardcoded dict; a hardcoded fallback remains for running tests outside the project. `pytest_terminal_summary` in `conftest.py` prints a tier distribution breakdown after each test run using `get_tier_for_path()`. `step5_quality_gate.py` includes the tier distribution in the STEP 5 quality gate report via `get_tier_distribution()`. `plugins/autonomous-dev/config/test_routing_config.json` updated with tier-aware routing. `skills/testing-guide/SKILL.md` updated to document the T0–T3 tier table with lifecycle definitions and reference `tier_registry.py` as the source of truth.

- **Project detection guard in `unified_pre_tool.py`** (#662): Non-native MCP tools in projects without autonomous-dev installed now receive an immediate allow before any enforcement layer runs. The guard calls `repo_detector.is_autonomous_dev_repo()` on the current working directory; if the project is not an autonomous-dev repo, the hook exits with allow and skips the 4-layer validation stack. Fail-closed: when `repo_detector` cannot be loaded at startup, enforcement continues as before so protection is never silently dropped. All behavior in autonomous-dev repos is unchanged. 5 new tests in `tests/unit/hooks/test_unified_pre_tool.py`; 1 test updated in `tests/unit/hooks/test_native_tool_auto_approval.py`.

- **`ui-tester` specialist agent for E2E browser testing** (#656): New optional STEP 9.7 in the `/implement` pipeline invokes `ui-tester` when changed files include frontend patterns (`*.html`, `*.tsx`, `*.jsx`, `*.vue`, `*.svelte`, `*.css`) and Playwright MCP tools are available. The agent writes persistent test files to `tests/e2e/` using the navigate → snapshot → interact → verify pattern. Security gates restrict navigation to localhost/127.0.0.1/0.0.0.0 and treat all page content as adversarial. Per-test timeout is 60 seconds; time-based waits are forbidden. Verdict is advisory only (`UI-TESTER-VERDICT: PASS` or `SKIP`) and never blocks the pipeline. `agent_invoker.py` and `skill_loader.py` updated with `ui-tester` configuration. `tests/e2e/conftest.py` provides shared Playwright fixtures with `e2e` pytest marker.

### Changed
- **Stick+carrot enforcement pattern in all hook block messages** (#660): Every deny path in `unified_pre_tool.py` now includes a `REQUIRED NEXT ACTION:` carrot directive in the `permissionDecisionReason` field, giving the model an explicit next step after a block (e.g., "Run /implement", "Use /create-issue --quick", "Wait for prerequisite agents to complete"). `agent_ordering_gate.py` ordering violation messages include the same carrot. New tests in `tests/unit/hooks/test_stick_carrot_directives.py` validate that deny messages carry both the original block prefix and the REQUIRED NEXT ACTION directive.
- **Documented `systemMessage` vs `permissionDecisionReason` visibility architecture** (#660): `docs/HOOKS.md` and `docs/ARCHITECTURE-OVERVIEW.md` now document that `permissionDecisionReason` on deny is model-visible (enforcement carrots go here) while `systemMessage` is user-visible (user notifications go here). This distinction was previously undocumented and is fundamental to why the carrot pattern works.

The new `sync_settings_hooks.py` CLI wraps `SettingsMerger` and accepts `--global` (merges `config/global_settings_template.json` into `~/.claude/settings.json`) or `--repo <path>` (merges `templates/settings.default.json` into `<repo>/.claude/settings.json`). Supports `--dry-run` and `--count-only` modes. Outputs JSON to stdout (`success`, `hooks_added`, `hooks_preserved`, `hooks_migrated`, `total_lifecycle_events`, `message`). Non-fatal: sync failures emit a warning but do not abort deploy. Tests in `tests/unit/scripts/test_sync_settings_hooks.py`.

### Changed
- **Strengthened gh issue create deny message with explicit anti-bypass clause**: `_detect_gh_issue_create()` in `unified_pre_tool.py` now includes a FORBIDDEN clause in the block message that explicitly forbids suggesting `! gh issue create` or any other bypass method. The `!` prefix runs commands outside the hook system and defeats enforcement. `docs/HOOKS.md` updated to reflect the expanded block message. 2 new assertions in `tests/unit/hooks/test_gh_issue_create_block.py` validate the FORBIDDEN language and `! gh issue create` text are present in the deny message.

- **Cluster selector for drain-queue tracker filtering** (Issue #1284): New pure function `select_next_cluster(clusters)` in `plugins/autonomous-dev/lib/cluster_selector.py` filters tracker-shaped issues from drain-queue candidates and prefers their lowest-numbered open leaf children when present. - **Hook-level pipeline agent ordering enforcement** (#625, #629, #632): `unified_pre_tool.py` gains a Pipeline Ordering Gate (Layer 4 for native Agent/Task tool calls) that blocks out-of-order agent invocations during active pipeline sessions. Two new libraries support this: `agent_ordering_gate.py` (pure-logic gate — no I/O, returns `GateResult` with `passed`, `reason`, and `missing_agents`) and `pipeline_completion_state.py` (per-session JSON state at `/tmp/pipeline_agent_completions_{hash}.json` with file locking, 2-hour auto-expiry, and `sequential`/`parallel` validation modes). `unified_session_tracker.py` records each agent's completion via `record_agent_completion()` on SubagentStop. The gate reads completion state at PreToolUse and blocks Agent/Task calls whose prerequisites have not been satisfied (e.g., implementer before planner or test-master). Supports parallel mode where the `reviewer → security-auditor` constraint is relaxed. Controlled by `PRE_TOOL_PIPELINE_ORDERING` env var (default: `true`); fails open on errors. 3 new test files: `tests/unit/lib/test_agent_ordering_gate.py`, `tests/unit/lib/test_pipeline_completion_state.py`, `tests/unit/hooks/test_pipeline_ordering_enforcement.py`.
- **Marker file removal from `/create-issue`, `/improve`, `/plan-to-issues`, `/refactor`, `/retrospective`** (#627): These commands no longer write `/tmp/autonomous_dev_gh_issue_allowed.marker`. The gh issue create gate Allow-through 3 (marker file) now applies only to the active `/implement` pipeline. Allow-through 4 (command context file at `/tmp/autonomous_dev_cmd_context.json`, added in #630) is the mechanism for these commands.

- **Plugin version stamping in session logs and auto-filed issues** (#630): New `version_reader.py` library provides `get_plugin_version()` returning `"X.Y.Z (abc1234)"` (with git SHA when available) or `"unknown"` on failure; result is module-level cached. `unified_session_tracker.py` now includes a `plugin_version` field in every JSONL session log entry. `continuous-improvement-analyst` TIMING issue bodies include the plugin version via inline `python3 -c` invocation. `/create-issue` and related commands (`/plan-to-issues`, `/improve`, `/refactor`, `/retrospective`) include the version stamp in auto-filed issue bodies. `tests/unit/lib/test_version_reader.py` added with comprehensive coverage.
- **Command context file mechanism for `/create-issue --quick` hook bypass** (#630): `unified_pre_tool.py` adds Allow-through 4 to the gh issue create gate — an active command context file at `/tmp/autonomous_dev_cmd_context.json` (valid for 1 hour, age checked via file mtime) with `command` set to one of `create-issue`, `plan-to-issues`, `improve`, `refactor`, or `retrospective` authorizes `gh issue create`. This complements the existing marker file (Allow-through 3) and enables `/create-issue --quick` from commands that cannot write the marker file. `tests/unit/hooks/test_gh_issue_command_context.py` added with full coverage.

- **`/implement` reuses research from `/create-issue` issue body** (#628): When given an issue reference (`#NNN`), `/implement` fetches the issue body at STEP 0 and calls `detect_issue_research()` at STEP 3 to check for embedded research sections. When three or more research-indicating H2 sections are detected (`is_research_rich=True`), STEP 4 research is skipped and the issue body content is passed directly to the planner at STEP 5. This eliminates redundant research for issues authored by `/create-issue`. `research_persistence.py` gains the new `detect_issue_research(issue_body)` public function with 12 unit tests in `tests/unit/lib/test_detect_issue_research.py`. The `--no-cache` flag overrides detection and forces fresh research.

### Changed
- **docs/LIBRARIES.md updated for Issue #628**: `research_persistence.py` entry updated with `detect_issue_research()` in Public API and Key Features sections. Line count updated from 700 to 901.

### Security
- **Direct creation of gh issue marker file blocked outside approved contexts** (#627): `unified_pre_tool.py` gains `_detect_gh_issue_marker_creation()`, which prevents Bash commands from directly writing `/tmp/autonomous_dev_gh_issue_allowed.marker`. Creating the file manually would short-circuit the entire gh issue create enforcement gate added in Issue #599. Detection anchors on the filename fragment `autonomous_dev_gh_issue_allowed` and covers `touch`, redirect `>`, `cp`, `mv`, `tee`, Python `Path.touch()`, `open(..., "w")`, and `.write_text()` patterns. Read-only and delete operations are not blocked. Allow-through conditions mirror the existing gh issue create gate: active `/implement` pipeline or an authorized agent (`continuous-improvement-analyst`, `issue-creator`). No marker-file allow-through exists for this check (circular). Fails open on any detection error. 20 unit tests in `tests/unit/hooks/test_gh_issue_create_block.py`.

### Fixed
- **doc-master now produces a verdict when pipeline has a second implementer pass** (#624): STEP 10 background doc-master ran against the pre-remediation file list when remediation occurred, producing a stale or missing verdict. STEP 12 now detects when STEP 11 remediation occurred, discards the stale background result, and re-invokes doc-master BLOCKING with a fresh `git diff` file list that reflects post-remediation changes. `agents/doc-master.md` updated with a note that the coordinator provides the current post-remediation file list. 7 unit tests in `tests/unit/test_doc_verdict_remediation.py`.

- **Automatic pipeline timing analysis via continuous-improvement-analyst** (#621): New `pipeline_timing_analyzer.py` library detects slow, wasteful, and ghost agent invocations from session logs. `extract_agent_timings(events)` pairs invocation/completion events by subagent type and computes wall-clock durations from timestamp deltas. `analyze_timings(timings, history_path)` applies per-agent static thresholds and adaptive p95-based thresholds (when 10+ historical observations exist), emitting `TimingFinding` entries with types `SLOW`, `WASTEFUL`, `GHOST`, and `SLOW_REGRESSION`. `save_timing_entry()` persists to a JSONL history file (thread-safe, capped at 20 entries per agent). `check_consecutive_violations()` requires 3 consecutive violations before an issue is filed (circuit breaker). `format_timing_report()` renders a CLI summary table. The continuous-improvement-analyst gains check #11 (Pipeline Timing Analysis) in Full Mode: runs the analyzer, deduplicates findings against open GitHub issues via find-or-create+comment, and caps new issue creation at 3 per run. 31 unit tests in `tests/unit/lib/test_pipeline_timing_analyzer.py`.

### Changed
- **docs/LIBRARIES.md updated for Issue #621**: `pipeline_timing_analyzer.py` added as Core Library entry 74. Core Libraries count updated from 73 to 74.
- **docs/ARCHITECTURE-OVERVIEW.md updated for Issue #621**: Component Counts library count updated from 180 to 181. `pipeline_timing_analyzer.py` added to Infrastructure key libraries.
- **docs/AGENTS.md updated for Issue #621**: `continuous-improvement-analyst` Detection Coverage section updated to reflect new timing analysis capability (check #11).

### Changed
- **Pipeline validation mode is now adaptive**: STEP 10 of `/implement` routes to parallel mode (all three agents — reviewer, security-auditor, doc-master — launched simultaneously) for low-risk changesets, and sequential mode (reviewer → security-auditor strict ordering, doc-master in background alongside reviewer) for security-sensitive files. `docs/AGENTS.md` and `docs/ARCHITECTURE-OVERVIEW.md` updated to reflect both modes; the previous description of exclusively sequential validation was stale.
- **Reviewer is static-only; no longer re-runs pytest**: The reviewer agent consumes STEP 8 test artifacts (pass/fail/skip counts, coverage) passed in context by the coordinator. It MUST NOT and does not re-execute pytest. `docs/AGENTS.md` reviewer execution description updated to clarify the test-artifact consumption model.
- **Auto-detect `--light` mode from file paths in STEP 0**: `/implement` now detects `--light` as a candidate when the feature description contains an explicit file path matching `*.md`, `*.json`, `*.yaml`, `*.yml`, `*.toml`, `docs/**`, `*.txt`, or `*.cfg` — unless a security-sensitive pattern is also present. Prompts user for confirmation before routing.
- **Research skipped for fully-specified changes (STEP 3.5)**: New STEP 3.5 detects fully-specified changes (specific file path + specific modification instruction, no security-sensitive topics). When detected, researcher-local and researcher agents are skipped, reducing pipeline time for simple targeted edits.

### Fixed
- **Infrastructure `.md` files now correctly blocked outside `/implement` pipeline** (#623): `_is_code_file_target()` in `unified_pre_tool.py` was applying the `.md` extension exemption before checking `_is_protected_infrastructure()`, allowing the coordinator to directly edit `agents/*.md`, `commands/*.md`, and `skills/*/SKILL.md` files during an active `/implement` session. The function now checks `_is_protected_infrastructure()` first — infrastructure `.md` files are classified as code targets regardless of extension, while regular docs (`README.md`, `docs/*.md`) remain unaffected by the protection. The same fix applies to Bash redirect detection. 7 regression tests added to `tests/unit/hooks/test_explicit_implement_enforcement.py`.
- **`research_persistence.py` research cache now saves and loads correctly** (#622): Path validation guard strings were checking for the wrong error message ("Path outside project" instead of "Path outside session directories"), causing `save_research()` and `load_cached_research()` to silently fail on valid research paths. The guard strings now match the actual error text emitted by `validate_session_path()`. Two regression tests added to `tests/unit/lib/test_research_persistence.py`. No API changes; no user-visible behavior changes beyond research cache now functioning correctly.
- **`_detect_gh_issue_create()` no longer produces false positives from commit messages or echo statements**: `unified_pre_tool.py` now calls `_strip_heredoc_content()` and `_strip_quoted_segments()` on the raw Bash command before applying the `gh issue create` regex. Previously, a `git commit -m "$(cat <<'EOF'...gh issue create...EOF)"` heredoc body or an `echo "run gh issue create manually"` quoted string would trigger the block even though no actual issue creation was occurring. The stripping step removes heredoc bodies and quoted string contents prior to pattern matching; genuine bare `gh issue create` calls remain fully blocked. The three allow-through conditions (active pipeline, authorized agent, marker file) are unchanged.
- **`/create-issue` marker file written at STEP 0 to unblock `issue-creator` agent**: The `gh issue create` enforcement hook requires `/tmp/autonomous_dev_gh_issue_allowed.marker` to exist when the `issue-creator` agent calls `gh issue create`. Previously the marker was only written inside STEP 3B (the same Bash block as `gh issue create`), which meant the hook saw no marker at the moment it evaluated the command and blocked it. The marker is now created at STEP 0 (argument parsing), before any agents are spawned, so the hook check passes. Cleanup path at CHECKPOINT 3 and the early-exit path (user chooses skip on duplicate prompt) are unchanged.

### Added
- **Cluster selector for drain-queue tracker filtering** (Issue #1284): New pure function `select_next_cluster(clusters)` in `plugins/autonomous-dev/lib/cluster_selector.py` filters tracker-shaped issues from drain-queue candidates and prefers their lowest-numbered open leaf children when present. Detects trackers via: title prefix `[TRACKER]`, label combination "root-cause" + "enhancement", or body structure with ≥2 phase headings + ≥2 issue references. When encountering a tracker with open leaves, returns a synthetic single-issue cluster pointing to the lowest leaf. Not yet wired into `/drain-queue` command — integration tracked separately. Added 5 unit tests in `tests/unit/lib/test_cluster_selector.py`.

- **Coordinator bypass verdict tracking** (Issue #1279): Extended `record_plan_critic_skipped()` with optional `bypass_reason` parameter and added new `write_coordinator_bypass_verdict()` function in `pipeline_completion_state.py` to enable drain-queue coordinators to record machine-readable audit trails when bypassing plan-critic for mechanical extensions. New `/drain-queue` command documentation section "Coordinator Bypass of plan-critic" details the two-call protocol. 3 new tests in `tests/regression/test_issue_1279_coordinator_bypass_verdict.py` verify the bypass tracking logic.
- **DOC-VERDICT-MISSING detection hardened to CRITICAL severity** (#602): New `doc_verdict_validator.py` library provides `validate_doc_verdict(doc_master_output)` for parsing `DOC-DRIFT-VERDICT` lines from doc-master output. Returns `DocVerdictResult` with `found`, `verdict` ("PASS"/"FAIL"/""), `finding_count` (N for FAIL(N), -1 if unparseable, 0 for PASS), `raw_line`, and `position_warning` (when verdict is not the final non-empty line). Strips ANSI escape codes before matching; handles `FAIL(0)` as PASS; uses the last verdict line when multiple appear. `pipeline_intent_validator.py` gains `detect_doc_verdict_missing()` function that correlates doc-master invocation/completion event pairs and emits CRITICAL findings when no completion exists, completion failed, or output is below `MIN_DOC_VERDICT_WORDS` (30). `agents/doc-master.md` strengthened with explicit HARD GATE requiring every run to end with a `DOC-DRIFT-VERDICT` line. 16 unit tests in `tests/unit/lib/test_doc_verdict_validator.py`.
- **Prompt compression prevention for batch mode** (#601, #603): New `prompt_integrity.py` library prevents progressive prompt compression in batch processing. `validate_prompt_word_count(agent_type, prompt, baseline)` checks emptiness, minimum word count for critical agents (security-auditor, reviewer: 80 words), and shrinkage against baseline (≤ 20% by default). `record_prompt_baseline()` / `get_prompt_baseline()` persist baselines to `.claude/logs/prompt_baselines.json` keyed by agent and issue number. `get_agent_prompt_template(agent_type)` reads the agent `.md` source for prompt reconstruction when compression is detected. `clear_prompt_baselines()` resets state at batch start. `implement-batch.md` gains REQUIRED prevention steps at STEP B3: first-issue baseline recording, per-subsequent-issue validation, and reload-from-disk on `should_reload=True`. 20 unit tests in `tests/unit/lib/test_prompt_integrity.py`.

### Changed
- **docs/LIBRARIES.md updated for Issue #602, #601/#603** (#602, #601/#603): `doc_verdict_validator.py` added as Core Library entry 72; `prompt_integrity.py` added as entry 73. Core Libraries count updated from 71 to 73. Last-updated date updated.
- **docs/BATCH-PROCESSING.md updated for prompt compression prevention** (#601, #603): New section "Prompt Integrity Across Issues" documents the HARD GATE, the `prompt_integrity.py` workflow (baseline recording, per-issue validation, reload-from-disk), and FORBIDDEN behaviors. `implement-batch.md` added to `covers:` frontmatter.
- **docs/ARCHITECTURE-OVERVIEW.md updated for new libraries** (#602, #601/#603): Component Counts library count updated from 178 to 180.

### Added
- **Cluster selector for drain-queue tracker filtering** (Issue #1284): New pure function `select_next_cluster(clusters)` in `plugins/autonomous-dev/lib/cluster_selector.py` filters tracker-shaped issues from drain-queue candidates and prefers their lowest-numbered open leaf children when present. Detects trackers via: title prefix `[TRACKER]`, label combination "root-cause" + "enhancement", or body structure with ≥2 phase headings + ≥2 issue references. When encountering a tracker with open leaves, returns a synthetic single-issue cluster pointing to the lowest leaf. Not yet wired into `/drain-queue` command — integration tracked separately. Added 5 unit tests in `tests/unit/lib/test_cluster_selector.py`.

- **Coordinator bypass verdict tracking** (Issue #1279): Extended `record_plan_critic_skipped()` with optional `bypass_reason` parameter and added new `write_coordinator_bypass_verdict()` function in `pipeline_completion_state.py` to enable drain-queue coordinators to record machine-readable audit trails when bypassing plan-critic for mechanical extensions. New `/drain-queue` command documentation section "Coordinator Bypass of plan-critic" details the two-call protocol. 3 new tests in `tests/regression/test_issue_1279_coordinator_bypass_verdict.py` verify the bypass tracking logic.
- **Prefix-based env var protection and escalation tracking in `unified_pre_tool.py`** (#606): `PROTECTED_ENV_PREFIXES` constant (`CLAUDE_` prefix) extends env-var spoofing detection beyond the individually listed pipeline variables so any newly introduced `CLAUDE_*` variable is protected without requiring an explicit addition to `PROTECTED_ENV_VARS`. `_is_protected_env_var()` unifies the individual-var and prefix-based checks into a single helper used by all three detection passes (inline assignment, `export`, `env` command). `_track_spoofing_escalation()` uses a file-based tracker at `~/.claude/logs/spoofing_attempts.json` to count spoofing attempts per session across separate hook invocations; when 2+ attempts occur in the same session the block message is escalated to signal a deliberate bypass attempt. 22 new unit tests added to `tests/unit/hooks/test_agent_identity_hardening.py` (65 total).

- **Cluster selector for drain-queue tracker filtering** (Issue #1284): New pure function `select_next_cluster(clusters)` in `plugins/autonomous-dev/lib/cluster_selector.py` filters tracker-shaped issues from drain-queue candidates and prefers their lowest-numbered open leaf children when present. New `/drain-queue` command documentation section "Coordinator Bypass of plan-critic" details the two-call protocol. 3 new tests in `tests/regression/test_issue_1279_coordinator_bypass_verdict.py` verify the bypass tracking logic.
- **Per-issue mode detection for `/implement --batch --issues`** (#600): New `batch_mode_detector.py` library analyzes issue title, body, and labels to automatically detect the appropriate pipeline mode (full/--fix/--light) for each issue in a batch run. Label overrides ("bug" -> --fix, "documentation" -> --light) take highest priority, followed by signal matching in title (2 pts) and body (1 pt), with fix winning ties. `github_issue_fetcher.py` gains `fetch_issue_details()` and `fetch_issues_details()` functions that fetch title+body+labels via `gh issue view --json title,body,labels`. `BatchState` dataclass gains `feature_modes` dict field (backward-compatible). `implement-batch.md` adds STEP I1.5 (Mode Detection and Confirmation) with summary table display and user override support. 38 unit tests in `tests/unit/lib/test_batch_mode_detector.py`.

### Added
- **Cluster selector for drain-queue tracker filtering** (Issue #1284): New pure function `select_next_cluster(clusters)` in `plugins/autonomous-dev/lib/cluster_selector.py` filters tracker-shaped issues from drain-queue candidates and prefers their lowest-numbered open leaf children when present. Detects trackers via: title prefix `[TRACKER]`, label combination "root-cause" + "enhancement", or body structure with ≥2 phase headings + ≥2 issue references. When encountering a tracker with open leaves, returns a synthetic single-issue cluster pointing to the lowest leaf. Not yet wired into `/drain-queue` command — integration tracked separately. Added 5 unit tests in `tests/unit/lib/test_cluster_selector.py`.

- **Coordinator bypass verdict tracking** (Issue #1279): Extended `record_plan_critic_skipped()` with optional `bypass_reason` parameter and added new `write_coordinator_bypass_verdict()` function in `pipeline_completion_state.py` to enable drain-queue coordinators to record machine-readable audit trails when bypassing plan-critic for mechanical extensions. New `/drain-queue` command documentation section "Coordinator Bypass of plan-critic" details the two-call protocol. 3 new tests in `tests/regression/test_issue_1279_coordinator_bypass_verdict.py` verify the bypass tracking logic.
- **`/retrospective` command for session drift detection** (#598): New command analyzes recent session activity logs to detect intent evolution, repeated user corrections, config drift (PROJECT.md/CLAUDE.md changes), and memory rot (stale entries with no recent corroboration). Produces tiered findings (IMMEDIATE/REVIEW/ARCHIVE) with proposed alignment edits as unified diffs. Supports `--sessions N`, `--dry-run`, `--auto-file`, and `--date YYYY-MM-DD` flags. Backed by `lib/retrospective_analyzer.py` library with resource limits (max 50 sessions, 200 events/session) and path validation. New `retrospective-analyst` agent categorizes findings and proposes edits without writing files directly. 31 unit tests in `tests/unit/lib/test_retrospective_analyzer.py`.
- **`gh issue create` blocked in Bash outside approved contexts** (#599): `unified_pre_tool.py` now detects direct `gh issue create` calls in Bash commands and blocks them to enforce the `/create-issue` pipeline (research, duplicate detection, proper formatting). Three allow-through conditions: (1) an active `/implement` pipeline is present, (2) the current agent is `continuous-improvement-analyst` or `issue-creator`, or (3) a fresh marker file at `/tmp/autonomous_dev_gh_issue_allowed.marker` (written by `/create-issue`, `/improve`, `/refactor`, `/plan-to-issues`) is present and under 1 hour old. Block message directs users to `/create-issue` or `/create-issue --quick`. Detection fails open so legitimate work is never blocked by an unexpected error. Unit tests in `tests/unit/hooks/test_gh_issue_create_block.py`; regression tests in `tests/regression/test_gh_issue_create_regression.py`.

### Fixed
- **4 bug fixes in `reviewer_benchmark.py`** (#568): (1) `load_dataset()` now wraps `json.JSONDecodeError` in `ValueError` that includes the file path and original parse error, making bad-JSON diagnostics actionable. (2) `scripts/run_reviewer_benchmark.py` now loads the dataset and handles `--validate-dataset` early-return before checking for `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`, so dataset validation no longer requires an API key. (3) `_VERDICT_BARE_RE` now uses `re.IGNORECASE` so bare verdict keywords (`approve`, `Blocking`, etc.) in the last 200 characters of a response are matched and uppercased correctly. (4) `_compute_per_category()` now groups results by each sample's `category_tags` when `sample_lookup` is provided (with multi-tag fan-out), falling back to `expected_verdict` grouping only when no lookup is available; previously, `per_category` always grouped by `expected_verdict` even when `samples` was passed to `score_results()`. 8 new regression tests added to `tests/unit/lib/test_reviewer_benchmark.py`. `docs/LIBRARIES.md` updated to reflect the new line count, accurate `parse_verdict` case-sensitivity behaviour, correct `score_results` `per_category` grouping description, and v1.1.1 version history entry.
- **`--validate-dataset` no longer requires `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`** (#574): The API key check in `scripts/run_reviewer_benchmark.py` previously ran before the `--validate-dataset` early-return path, causing the script to exit with an error when the key was absent even though dataset validation makes no API calls. Reordered so the key check occurs only after the validate-only branch exits. Two regression tests added to `tests/unit/lib/test_reviewer_benchmark.py` confirm that `--validate-dataset` succeeds without the key and that a normal benchmark run without the key still fails with a clear error.
- **`scripts/mine_session_logs.py` validates `--logs-dir` and `--output` paths before use** (#575): Added `validate_path()` calls (from `security_utils`) at CLI entry in `main()` so invalid or path-traversal `--logs-dir` and `--output` arguments are rejected with a clear error message before any file I/O occurs. Previously the script would silently accept any path and fail later with an unhelpful OS error. Test coverage added in `tests/unit/scripts/test_mine_session_logs.py`.


- **AgentTracker no longer raises `KeyError: 'agents'` when loading session files that omit the key** (#576): Pipeline session files written with the new schema did not include an `agents` key, causing `AgentTracker.__init__` to crash immediately on load. Added `setdefault("agents", [])` at all three session-file loading sites in `tracker.py` (explicit-path branch, `CLAUDE_SESSION_ID`-matched branch, and no-session-ID fallback branch). New regression test file `tests/unit/test_agent_tracker_new_schema.py` covers both old-schema (with key) and new-schema (without key) session files across all loading paths.
- **Eliminated double state-file read in `validate_agent_authorization()`** (#586): `_is_explicit_implement_active()` was called twice in the same evaluation path inside `validate_agent_authorization()` — once for the alignment gate check and once for the workflow enforcement check. Extracted the result into a local `impl_active` variable so the state file is read exactly once per invocation. Two regression tests added to `test_explicit_implement_enforcement.py` verify the call count stays at one regardless of which branch is taken.
- **Session isolation tests now import production `unified_session_tracker` directly** (#595): `tests/unit/hooks/test_unified_session_tracker_isolation.py` previously imported an internal replica of `unified_session_tracker` rather than the production hook module. Tests have been updated to import from `plugins/autonomous-dev/hooks/unified_session_tracker` so that the 5 session-isolation tests (Issue #594) actually validate the production code path rather than a copy that could diverge silently.
- **`EXPECTED_PIPELINE_AGENTS` exported as named module-level constant** (#597): `unified_session_tracker.py` previously embedded the pipeline agent list as an inline literal inside `check_pipeline_complete()`. The list is now a module-level constant `EXPECTED_PIPELINE_AGENTS` so tests can import the authoritative value directly rather than maintaining a duplicate. `test_unified_session_tracker_isolation.py` updated to import from the production module, ensuring tests stay in sync automatically when the agent list changes.
- **Cross-session pipeline.json mixing no longer causes false completions or stale state** (#594): `AgentTracker` now reads `CLAUDE_SESSION_ID` at initialization and, when set, filters candidate `pipeline.json` files to those whose stored `claude_session_id` field matches the current session. If no matching file exists a new one is created; the fallback to the latest file is retained for backward compatibility when the env var is absent. `unified_session_tracker.py` applies the same filter in both `SessionTracker` (docs/sessions/*.md file selection) and `check_pipeline_complete()` (pipeline completion check), preventing a completing session from reading another session's pipeline state. Session files now include a `claude_session_id` field in their JSON structure. 6 new unit tests in `tests/unit/lib/test_agent_tracker_session_isolation.py` and 5 new unit tests in `tests/unit/hooks/test_unified_session_tracker_isolation.py`.


- **Pipeline intent validator no longer produces false INCOMPLETE findings in worktree context** (#593): When `validate_pipeline_intent()` ran inside a git worktree, session activity logs written by `session_activity_logger.py` were often stored in the main repository's `.claude/logs/activity/` directory rather than the worktree's own directory. Because the validator only read the local log path, pipeline events from the current session appeared absent and triggered spurious INCOMPLETE findings. `path_utils.get_main_repo_activity_log_dir()` was added to resolve the parent repository's activity log directory when running inside a worktree. `parse_session_logs()` gains an `additional_log_paths` parameter to merge events from supplemental JSONL files; duplicates are removed by `(timestamp, tool, session_id)` key before sorting. `validate_pipeline_intent()` automatically calls `get_main_repo_activity_log_dir()` and passes the matching log file as `additional_log_paths`, merging both log sources transparently. 9 new unit tests in `tests/unit/lib/test_worktree_log_resolution.py`.

- **False-positive env-var export detection no longer blocks CI analyst from filing GitHub issues** (#590): `_detect_env_spoofing()` in `unified_pre_tool.py` previously matched protected variable names (e.g. `CLAUDE_AGENT_NAME`) anywhere in the command string, including inside quoted `--body` arguments. When the continuous-improvement-analyst used `gh issue create --body "...CLAUDE_AGENT_NAME appeared in the session log..."` to file a GitHub issue with evidence text, the hook incorrectly treated the quoted argument value as an attempted identity spoof and blocked the command. Added `_strip_quoted_segments()` helper (Issue #590) that removes single- and double-quoted segments from the command string before pattern matching; `_detect_env_spoofing()` now operates on the stripped string. Actual inline spoofing patterns (`CLAUDE_AGENT_NAME=value cmd`) remain fully blocked; only quoted string contents are exempt. 12 new unit tests in `tests/unit/hooks/test_agent_identity_hardening.py` cover the false-positive scenarios alongside continued blocking of genuine spoof attempts.
- **Stale `implement_pipeline_state.json` from a prior session no longer blocks Bash writes** (#592): When a Claude session ended without cleanup, the leftover `/tmp/implement_pipeline_state.json` would be picked up by the next session's `unified_pre_tool.py`, which mistook it for an active pipeline and denied all infrastructure Bash writes. Added `_is_stale_session()` helper that compares the `session_id` stored in the state file against the current session's `CLAUDE_SESSION_ID`; when they differ (and both are known), the stale file is removed and the hook falls through to normal decision logic. `implement.md` pipeline activation code now writes `session_id` into the state dict at creation time so subsequent hooks can detect staleness. Staleness checks are applied in all three `_is_pipeline_active()` call sites that read the state file. 14 new unit tests in `tests/unit/hooks/test_stale_pipeline_state.py` cover stale detection, same-session pass-through, missing/unknown session_id fallback, and file removal. `tests/unit/hooks/test_unified_pre_tool.py` updated with test isolation fixes.
- **Pipeline subagents no longer denied writes due to absent `CLAUDE_AGENT_NAME` in subprocess env** (#591): `unified_pre_tool.py` previously resolved agent identity exclusively from the `CLAUDE_AGENT_NAME` environment variable. When the hook fires inside a subagent process, Claude Code does not propagate `CLAUDE_AGENT_NAME` to the subprocess environment, causing authorized pipeline agents (implementer, reviewer, etc.) to fail the agent-auth layer and have their writes blocked. Added `_get_active_agent_name()` helper that reads `agent_type` from the hook stdin JSON payload as the primary identity source (Claude Code always populates this field inside subagents) and falls back to `CLAUDE_AGENT_NAME` for compatibility. `_agent_type` module-level variable is extracted from stdin in `main()` and consumed by `_get_active_agent_name()` at all four former `os.getenv("CLAUDE_AGENT_NAME")` call sites. 8 new unit tests in `tests/unit/hooks/test_unified_pre_tool.py` cover the stdin-primary, env-fallback, and absent-identity paths.
- **Regression tests lock in Issue #572 Write-to-/tmp + cp bypass** (#572): Issue #572 identified that the Write tool correctly denied a direct write to `lib/reviewer_benchmark.py`, but the guard could be circumvented by writing staged content to `/tmp` first and then running `cp /tmp/staged_payload.py lib/reviewer_benchmark.py`. The cp/mv destination detection introduced in #589 covers this exact pattern. Five targeted regression tests in `tests/regression/test_bash_write_bypass_regression.py` (`TestIssue572WriteToTmpThenCp`) lock in this bypass route: exact cp command is extracted by `_extract_bash_file_writes`, blocked end-to-end by `_check_bash_infra_writes`, classified as a code-file target by `_is_code_file_target`; mv variant is also blocked; writes to `/tmp` itself remain allowed (precision check). These tests prevent silent regression of the fix that #589 delivered for this specific attack vector.
- **Bash write bypass via `Path.write_text()` and `sed -i` now fully blocked** (#589): `_extract_bash_file_writes()` previously only detected shell redirects (`>`, `>>`), `tee`, `cat > file << EOF`, and `dd of=FILE`. Wrapping a write in `python3 -c "Path('hooks/foo.py').write_text(...)"`, `sed -i`, `cp`/`mv`, or a python3 heredoc bypassed the code-file enforcement check in `_is_code_file_in_bash()`, even though `_check_bash_infra_writes()` caught these for infrastructure paths. Extended `_extract_bash_file_writes()` with four new detection patterns (sed -i in-place edits; cp/mv destination paths; python3 -c with `open()` write mode or `Path.write_text/write_bytes`; python3 heredoc bodies with the same patterns) so all Bash-wrapped writes are now caught by both enforcement paths. Python snippet analysis is backed by the new `python_write_detector.py` library (AST-based with regex fallback; handles aliased `Path` imports, `shutil.copy/move` destinations, and dynamic `eval()`/`exec()` detection). 12 new unit tests in `tests/unit/hooks/test_bash_write_bypass.py` and 17 regression tests in `tests/regression/test_bash_write_bypass_regression.py` (expanded from 2 by #572). Library count updated to 178.
- **`python_write_detector.py` added as shared library** (#589): Extracted Python write-detection logic into `plugins/autonomous-dev/lib/python_write_detector.py`. Public API: `extract_write_targets(code)` (AST with regex fallback), `extract_write_targets_ast(code)`, `extract_write_targets_regex(code)`, `has_suspicious_exec(code)`, `SUSPICIOUS_EXEC_SENTINEL` constant. Detects `Path.write_text/write_bytes` with any import alias, `open()` in write/append mode, `shutil.copy/copy2/move/copyfile` destinations, and `eval()`/`exec()` with non-constant arguments. `unified_pre_tool.py` imports it defensively at startup and falls back to inline regex if the module is unavailable. Unit tests in `tests/unit/lib/test_python_write_detector.py`.
