New `/drain-queue` command documentation section "Coordinator Bypass of plan-critic" details the two-call protocol. 3 new tests in `tests/regression/test_issue_1279_coordinator_bypass_verdict.py` verify the bypass tracking logic.
- **Runtime Verification Classifier** (#564): Added `runtime_verification_classifier.py` — classifies changed files into runtime verification categories (frontend, API, CLI) so the reviewer agent can perform targeted runtime checks after completing static code review. `classify_runtime_targets(file_paths)` returns a `RuntimeVerificationPlan` with a `has_targets` flag and typed target lists (`FrontendTarget`, `ApiTarget`, `CliTarget`). Frontend detection matches `.html`, `.tsx`, `.jsx`, `.vue`, `.svelte` files; API detection matches `routes/`, `api/`, `endpoints/`, `views/` path patterns and common server filenames with framework guessing (fastapi, flask, express); CLI detection matches scripts and named CLI tools. All detection excludes test files. `reviewer.md` updated with a **Runtime Verification (Opt-In)** section specifying the HARD GATE (runtime verification MUST NOT run when static review has BLOCKING findings), 60-second total time cap, `timeout 30` wrapper requirement, and per-target-type check guidance (Playwright MCP for frontend, curl for API, subprocess for CLI). `implement.md` STEP 10a updated with a note that the reviewer MAY perform targeted runtime verification after static review. Library count updated to 177. Unit tests: `tests/unit/lib/test_runtime_verification_classifier.py`. Acceptance tests: `tests/genai/test_acceptance_runtime_verification.py`.

### Fixed
- **VERSION file congruence with PROJECT.md**: `plugins/autonomous-dev/VERSION` updated from 3.50.0 to 3.51.0 to match the version declared in PROJECT.md.
- **Session-scoped pipeline ordering validation** (#587): `validate_pipeline_intent()` in `pipeline_intent_validator.py` previously treated all events in a shared daily JSONL log as a single sequence, producing false CRITICAL `step_ordering` findings whenever two different sessions happened to run agents out of order relative to each other. Events are now grouped by `session_id` before any check functions are applied; each session's events are validated independently. Legacy events without a `session_id` field are grouped into a shared fallback session for backward compatibility. `PipelineEvent` dataclass gains a `session_id: str` field populated from JSONL entries. The internal `_run_checks_on_events()` helper was extracted to make per-session dispatch clean.

### Added
- **Cluster selector for drain-queue tracker filtering** (Issue #1284): New pure function `select_next_cluster(clusters)` in `plugins/autonomous-dev/lib/cluster_selector.py` filters tracker-shaped issues from drain-queue candidates and prefers their lowest-numbered open leaf children when present. Detects trackers via: title prefix `[TRACKER]`, label combination "root-cause" + "enhancement", or body structure with ≥2 phase headings + ≥2 issue references. When encountering a tracker with open leaves, returns a synthetic single-issue cluster pointing to the lowest leaf. Not yet wired into `/drain-queue` command — integration tracked separately. Added 5 unit tests in `tests/unit/lib/test_cluster_selector.py`.

- **Coordinator bypass verdict tracking** (Issue #1279): Extended `record_plan_critic_skipped()` with optional `bypass_reason` parameter and added new `write_coordinator_bypass_verdict()` function in `pipeline_completion_state.py` to enable drain-queue coordinators to record machine-readable audit trails when bypassing plan-critic for mechanical extensions. New `/drain-queue` command documentation section "Coordinator Bypass of plan-critic" details the two-call protocol. 3 new tests in `tests/regression/test_issue_1279_coordinator_bypass_verdict.py` verify the bypass tracking logic.
- **Component Classification Registry** (#566): Central JSON registry at `plugins/autonomous-dev/config/component_classifications.json` classifies all enforcement components (hooks, HARD GATEs, FORBIDDEN lists) as `model-limitation` or `process-requirement`. Model-limitation components exist because the model exhibits specific failure patterns and include concrete `removal_criteria` for future removal when model capabilities improve. Process-requirement components enforce engineering, security, or operational standards independent of model capability and are never removed for model-improvement reasons alone. JSON Schema at `plugins/autonomous-dev/config/component_classifications.schema.json` validates registry completeness. Validation script at `scripts/validate_component_classifications.py` ensures no components are unclassified. Documentation at `docs/HARNESS-EVOLUTION.md` describes the classification system, review process, and model upgrade checklist. Unit tests in `tests/unit/config/test_component_classifications.py`; acceptance tests in `tests/genai/test_acceptance_component_classifications.py`.
- **Alignment Gate: Hook-Level Enforcement + Scope Injection** (#585): `unified_pre_tool.py` now blocks coordinator Write/Edit/Bash to code files when `/implement` is active but STEP 2 (PROJECT.md alignment) has not yet completed — `alignment_passed: true` must be present in the pipeline state before any code changes are permitted. The gate fails closed on HMAC failure or missing state. `alignment_passed` is included in the HMAC-signed fields of `pipeline_state.py` to prevent state tampering. `implement.md` STEP 2 updated to write `alignment_passed: True` to the pipeline state after the alignment check passes. `implement.md` STEP 5 (planner) and STEP 10a (reviewer) now receive PROJECT.md GOALS and SCOPE sections verbatim so sub-agents operate within validated scope boundaries. 12 unit tests (`test_alignment_gate_enforcement.py`), 7 library tests (`test_pipeline_state_alignment.py`), and 11 acceptance tests (`test_acceptance_alignment_gate.py`).

### Fixed
- **Policy schema regression restored to v2.0** (#561, #562): `auto_approve_policy.json` was reverted to v1.0 (whitelist-first) schema during conflict resolution in commit 9b42c0d. Restored to v2.0 (blacklist-first, permissive mode) in both `plugins/autonomous-dev/config/` and `.claude/config/`. Schema validation tests added to `test_native_tool_auto_approval.py` to prevent future regressions. `allowed-tools` frontmatter added to `implement-fix.md`, `refactor.md`, and `sweep.md` command files for enforcement compatibility.
- **TOOL-AUTO-APPROVAL.md Configuration section** updated: Policy File "Structure" example now shows v2.0 blacklist-first schema instead of stale v1.0 whitelist-first schema.
- **Deploy script no longer deletes hook extensions** (#560): `scripts/deploy-all.sh` rsync `--delete` runs on the hooks directory previously wiped the `extensions/` subdirectory on every deploy, destroying user-authored and project-specific hook extensions. Fixed by adding `--exclude=extensions/` to all rsync `--delete` commands and adding `plugins/autonomous-dev/hooks/extensions/.gitkeep` so the directory is tracked in source and treated as a valid sync target rather than an orphan.

### Added
- **Cluster selector for drain-queue tracker filtering** (Issue #1284): New pure function `select_next_cluster(clusters)` in `plugins/autonomous-dev/lib/cluster_selector.py` filters tracker-shaped issues from drain-queue candidates and prefers their lowest-numbered open leaf children when present. Detects trackers via: title prefix `[TRACKER]`, label combination "root-cause" + "enhancement", or body structure with ≥2 phase headings + ≥2 issue references. When encountering a tracker with open leaves, returns a synthetic single-issue cluster pointing to the lowest leaf. Not yet wired into `/drain-queue` command — integration tracked separately. Added 5 unit tests in `tests/unit/lib/test_cluster_selector.py`.

- **Coordinator bypass verdict tracking** (Issue #1279): Extended `record_plan_critic_skipped()` with optional `bypass_reason` parameter and added new `write_coordinator_bypass_verdict()` function in `pipeline_completion_state.py` to enable drain-queue coordinators to record machine-readable audit trails when bypassing plan-critic for mechanical extensions. New `/drain-queue` command documentation section "Coordinator Bypass of plan-critic" details the two-call protocol. 3 new tests in `tests/regression/test_issue_1279_coordinator_bypass_verdict.py` verify the bypass tracking logic.
- **Runtime Data Aggregator** (#579, Component 1): Added `runtime_data_aggregator.py` — collects improvement signals from 4 sources (session activity logs, benchmark history, CI bypass patterns, GitHub issues), normalizes severity, computes cross-source priority via `SEVERITY_WEIGHTS × severity × log(1+frequency)`, ranks signals, and persists ranked reports as append-only JSONL at `.claude/logs/aggregated_reports.jsonl`. Key classes: `AggregatedSignal`, `SourceHealth`, `AggregatedReport`. Security hardening: CWE-532 secret scrubbing, CWE-400 line cap (MAX_LINES=100,000), CWE-78 subprocess argument lists, CWE-22 path validation. Unit tests: `tests/unit/lib/test_runtime_data_aggregator.py`. Acceptance tests: `tests/genai/test_acceptance_runtime_data_aggregator.py`. Library count updated to 176.
- **Automated Reviewer Improvement Loop** (#578): Added `scripts/improve_reviewer.py` — a CLI tool that runs a baseline benchmark, analyzes reviewer weaknesses via `reviewer_weakness_analyzer.py`, generates improvement instructions, applies them to the `## Learned Patterns` section of `reviewer.md`, runs a verification benchmark, checks for per-category regressions (default threshold 5%), and commits on success (or reverts on regression). New libraries: `benchmark_history.py` (append-only JSONL trend tracking with `BenchmarkHistory.append/load_all/load_latest/trend`) and `reviewer_weakness_analyzer.py` (`analyze_weaknesses`, `generate_improvement_instructions`, failure-mode weights). `reviewer.md` gains `## Evaluation Criteria` (taxonomy group → review check mapping) and `## Learned Patterns` (auto-populated by the improvement loop). `implementer.md` gains quality criteria section. `scripts/run_reviewer_benchmark.py` gains `--history` flag for JSONL trend logging. Unit tests: `test_benchmark_history.py`, `test_reviewer_weakness_analyzer.py`, `test_improve_reviewer.py`. GenAI acceptance tests: `test_acceptance_reviewer_improvement.py`. See [docs/LIBRARIES.md](docs/LIBRARIES.md) for library API reference.
- **Benchmark Expansion to 146 Samples with 91-Category Taxonomy** (#573): Expanded `tests/benchmarks/reviewer/dataset.json` from 14 to 146 labeled samples with difficulty stratification (easy/medium/hard). Added `tests/benchmarks/reviewer/taxonomy.json` with 91 defect categories. `reviewer_benchmark.py` updated with `difficulty`, `commit_sha`, and `defect_category` fields on `BenchmarkSample`; `ScoringReport` gains `per_difficulty` and `per_defect_category` breakdown dicts; `score_results()` now accepts optional `samples` parameter to populate those breakdowns. New mining scripts: `scripts/mine_git_samples.py` (git history mining) and `scripts/mine_session_logs.py` (session log mining). CLI flags `--filter-difficulty`, `--filter-category`, and `--validate-dataset` added to `scripts/run_reviewer_benchmark.py`. 29 acceptance tests, 10 new unit tests for the library, and 49 new unit tests for the mining scripts.
- **Reviewer Effectiveness Benchmark Suite** (#567): Added `reviewer_benchmark.py` library with dataset loader, prompt builder, verdict parser, and scoring engine (balanced accuracy, FPR, FNR, consistency rate). CLI runner at `scripts/run_reviewer_benchmark.py`. Labeled dataset at `tests/benchmarks/reviewer/dataset.json`. Unit tests at `tests/unit/lib/test_reviewer_benchmark.py`. Benchmark results directories excluded from git. Library count updated to 173. See [docs/LIBRARIES.md](docs/LIBRARIES.md) for full API reference.
- **Prompt Reload Guidance in Compression Findings** (#561): `Finding` dataclass gains `recommended_action: Optional[str]` field. `detect_progressive_compression()` now accepts optional `agents_dir: Path` parameter and populates `recommended_action` with a specific prompt reload instruction (e.g. "Reload prompt from agents/reviewer.md before processing issue #N"). `get_minimum_prompt_content(agent_type, agents_dir)` reads the first ~200 words of an agent's `.md` file as a minimum prompt baseline; validates `agent_type` against `VALID_AGENT_TYPES` whitelist before constructing paths. `VALID_AGENT_TYPES` constant derived from `STEP_ORDER` keys.
- **Accurate Doc-Verdict Detection via Invocation/Completion Correlation** (#562): `detect_doc_verdict_missing()` rewritten to use new `_correlate_invocation_completion()` helper, which pairs PostToolUse `agent_invocation` events with SubagentStop `agent_completion` events by subagent type and temporal proximity (10-minute window, earliest-available matching). PostToolUse events always carry `result_word_count=0`; the completion event carries the actual output word count. Detection now correctly flags: no matching completion (timeout/crash), failed completion (`success=False`), or output below `MIN_DOC_VERDICT_WORDS = 30`.
- **Batch CIA Skip Detection** (#559): Added `detect_batch_cia_skip()` to `pipeline_intent_validator.py`. Detects batch issues where `continuous-improvement-analyst` was not invoked. Last-issue skips are flagged as WARNING (known regression, Issue #505); non-last-issue skips are flagged as INFO. Integrated into `validate_pipeline_intent()` orchestrator.
- **Per-Issue Doc-Drift Verdict Collection** (#559): Batch mode (`implement-batch.md` STEP B3) now parses and acts on `DOC-DRIFT-VERDICT` for each issue as doc-master completes, mirroring single-issue verdict handling. FAIL blocks advancement to the next issue; missing verdict triggers one retry with reduced context.

### Changed
- **Batch required agent count updated** (#559): Acceptance-first mode now requires 8 agents per issue (was 7) — `continuous-improvement-analyst` is now a required agent rather than optional post-issue QA. TDD-first mode requires 9 agents (was 8). The per-issue HARD GATE in `implement-batch.md` STEP B3 enforces this for every issue including the last.
- **STEP 15 cleanup ordering fixed** (#559): `implement.md` now enforces that pipeline state cleanup occurs only after the STEP 15 continuous-improvement-analyst launch is confirmed, preventing the analyst from losing context due to premature cleanup.

### Added
- **Cluster selector for drain-queue tracker filtering** (Issue #1284): New pure function `select_next_cluster(clusters)` in `plugins/autonomous-dev/lib/cluster_selector.py` filters tracker-shaped issues from drain-queue candidates and prefers their lowest-numbered open leaf children when present. Detects trackers via: title prefix `[TRACKER]`, label combination "root-cause" + "enhancement", or body structure with ≥2 phase headings + ≥2 issue references. When encountering a tracker with open leaves, returns a synthetic single-issue cluster pointing to the lowest leaf. Not yet wired into `/drain-queue` command — integration tracked separately. Added 5 unit tests in `tests/unit/lib/test_cluster_selector.py`.

- **Coordinator bypass verdict tracking** (Issue #1279): Extended `record_plan_critic_skipped()` with optional `bypass_reason` parameter and added new `write_coordinator_bypass_verdict()` function in `pipeline_completion_state.py` to enable drain-queue coordinators to record machine-readable audit trails when bypassing plan-critic for mechanical extensions. New `/drain-queue` command documentation section "Coordinator Bypass of plan-critic" details the two-call protocol. 3 new tests in `tests/regression/test_issue_1279_coordinator_bypass_verdict.py` verify the bypass tracking logic.
- **CI and Pre-Commit Hook Enforcement** (#555): Hook sidecar consistency is now enforced at three levels: (1) CI runs `generate_hook_config.py --check` in the smoke job, failing the build on drift; (2) `scripts/pre-commit-hook-check.sh` validates sidecars when hook-related files are staged; (3) `/sync --verify` provides manual consistency checking. See [docs/HOOK-SIDECAR-SCHEMA.md](docs/HOOK-SIDECAR-SCHEMA.md#enforcement) for the developer workflow.
- **Hook Sidecar Migration Complete** (#554): All 25 active hooks now have `.hook.json` sidecar registration files. Includes 8 lifecycle hooks (plan_mode_exit_detector, post_compact_enricher, pre_compact_batch_saver, session_activity_logger, task_completed_handler, unified_pre_tool, unified_prompt_validator, unified_session_tracker) and 17 utility hooks (auto_fix_docs, auto_format, auto_test, enforce_orchestrator, enforce_tdd, genai_prompts, genai_utils, PreToolUseWrite-protect-sensitive, security_scan, SessionStart-batch-recovery, setup, stop_quality_gate, UserPromptSubmit-orchestrator, UserPromptSubmit-track-issues, validate_command_file_ops, validate_project_alignment, validate_session_quality). All sidecars validate against `hook-metadata.schema.json` and enable declarative hook management.
- **Hook Configuration Generator** (#552): Added `scripts/generate_hook_config.py` for automated hook config generation from `.hook.json` sidecars. Supports `--check` mode (report drift without modifying) and `--write` mode (update install_manifest.json and global_settings_template.json). Verbose output with `-v` flag shows exactly what changed. Validates sidecars against `hook-metadata.schema.json`, detects orphan hooks/sidecars, groups registrations by event, and maintains deterministic ordering. Exit codes: 0 (success), 1 (drift/errors), 2 (CLI errors). Usage examples and exit codes documented in [docs/HOOK-SIDECAR-SCHEMA.md](docs/HOOK-SIDECAR-SCHEMA.md#configuration-generator).
- **Hook Sidecar Metadata Schema** (#551, Part 1/5): Introduced `.hook.json` sidecar files for declarative hook registration. Each hook (lifecycle or utility) can now have a companion JSON metadata file declaring lifecycle events, tool matchers, timeouts, environment variables, and type semantics. Eliminates manual registration in settings templates. Schema: `plugins/autonomous-dev/config/hook-metadata.schema.json` (JSON Schema draft 2020-12). Detailed guide at [docs/HOOK-SIDECAR-SCHEMA.md](docs/HOOK-SIDECAR-SCHEMA.md). Enables automated settings generation and validation in subsequent parts of the series.
- **Sync Integration for Hook Config Generator** (#553): `/sync --github` and `/sync --plugin-dev` now automatically run the hook config generator after deploying hooks. Adds `--no-generate` flag for emergency bypass if generator needs to be skipped. The `--all` mode also triggers generation since it runs github and plugin-dev modes.

### Changed
- **Validation Escalation for Missing Hooks** (#553): Post-sync validation now escalates missing hook references from warnings to errors for object-format settings (modern `{"PreToolUse": [...]}` format). Missing hook files are critical and must be resolved before proceeding. Legacy array-format settings retain warning-level reporting.
- **Prompt Integrity Detection** (#544): Added `detect_progressive_compression()` and `detect_minimum_prompt_violation()` functions to pipeline_intent_validator for detecting prompt shrinkage across batch issues. Security-critical agents (reviewer, security-auditor) now validated against minimum 80-word thresholds and 25% maximum shrinkage ratio between issues.
- **Doc-Verdict-Missing Detection** (#543): Added `detect_doc_verdict_missing()` function to pipeline_intent_validator to detect doc-master timeouts or failures. Implemented retry-with-reduced-context protocol in STEP 12 of implement.md when doc-master produces empty output or crashes, ensuring documentation drift is not missed due to agent timeout.
- Property-based tests (Issue #509): `tests/property/` directory with three Hypothesis test modules covering `security_utils` path traversal and agent name invariants, `pipeline_state` state machine invariants (creation, step re-entry prevention, skippable step enforcement, round-trip persistence), and `tool_validator` classification invariants (blacklist, injection patterns, always-allowed tools). Tests auto-marked `property` + `slow` via `tests/conftest.py` directory marker. `property` marker registered in `pytest.ini`.
- Smart test routing (Issue #508): `/implement` STEP 5 now classifies changed files into categories (hook, lib, agent_prompt, command, config, skill, docs_only, install_sync) and runs only the relevant pytest marker tiers instead of the full suite. Docs-only changes skip tests entirely. Pass `--full-tests` to bypass routing and run the complete suite.
- `test_routing.py` library: `route_tests()` high-level API plus `classify_changes()`, `compute_marker_expression()`, and `get_skipped_tiers()` for per-category test tier selection.
- `step5_quality_gate.py` library: `run_quality_gate()` consolidates test execution (with smart routing), coverage regression check, and skip baseline enforcement into a single STEP 5 gate. `run_tests_routed()` delegates to `test_routing` when available and falls back to the full suite.
- `test_routing_config.json` config: routing rules per change category, tier-to-marker mappings, `always_smoke` and `docs_only_skip_all` flags — all user-tunable without code changes.
- `/refactor` command: unified code, docs, and test optimization with deeper analysis than `/sweep`. Three modes: `--tests` (Quality Diamond shape + waste detection), `--docs-redundancy` (redundancy via SequenceMatcher; was `--docs` before Issue #1098 rename), `--code` (dead code + unused lib detection). Supports `--fix` to apply fixes and `--quick` for the original sweep-style hygiene check.
- `refactor_analyzer.py` library: `RefactorAnalyzer` class orchestrating deep analysis across test shape, test waste, doc redundancy, dead code, and unused libraries. Composes `SweepAnalyzer` for quick-sweep mode.
- `ConfidenceLevel` enum (`HIGH`/`MEDIUM`/`LOW`) added to `refactor_analyzer.py`; `RefactorFinding` now carries a `confidence` field (default `HIGH`) so callers can filter findings by reliability.
- `/refactor --fix` now applies confidence-level gating: HIGH-confidence findings are passed to agents for automated fix, MEDIUM-confidence findings are surfaced as a "Manual Review Recommended" list (not auto-fixed), and LOW-confidence findings are excluded from `--fix` output entirely.
- Dead code and unused library detection now use word-boundary regex (`\b<name>\b`) to avoid false positives from partial name matches.
- Unused library detection now scans `.md` and `.sh` files in addition to `.py` files, reducing false positives where a lib is only referenced in documentation or shell scripts.
- `genai_refactor_analyzer.py` library: `GenAIRefactorAnalyzer` wraps `RefactorAnalyzer` with hybrid static-candidate + LLM-semantic analysis. Three passes: (1) doc-code drift via `covers:` frontmatter with Haiku contradiction check and Sonnet escalation for HIGH findings, (2) hollow test detection pairing `test_foo.py` with `foo.py`, (3) dead code verification with dynamic dispatch context and confidence threshold (≥0.7). SHA-256 content hash caching at `.claude/cache/refactor/` prevents redundant API calls. (Issue #515)
- `/refactor --deep` flag: enables `GenAIRefactorAnalyzer` for semantic analysis (auto-enabled when `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` is set and `--quick` is not). Findings prefixed with `[genai]` indicate higher-confidence semantic-level issues beyond structural detection.
- `/refactor --issues` flag: pipes findings into GitHub issues via `gh issue create` — HIGH/CRITICAL findings get individual issues, LOW/MEDIUM findings are aggregated into a single sweep issue.
- `/refactor --batch` flag: submits GenAI analysis via Anthropic Batch API for 50% cost reduction with async results.
- 5 new GenAI prompts added to `genai_prompts.py`: `DOC_CODE_DRIFT_PROMPT`, `HOLLOW_TEST_PROMPT`, `DEAD_CODE_VERIFY_PROMPT`, `REFACTOR_ESCALATION_PROMPT`, `REFACTOR_BATCH_SYSTEM_PROMPT` — structured prompts for each semantic analysis pass.

### Changed
- `/sweep` is now an alias for `/refactor --quick` instead of running independent analysis logic. Behavior is preserved; all hygiene sweep work is delegated to `RefactorAnalyzer.quick_sweep()`.
- `pipeline_state.py` `finalize_to_session()` now accepts `feature_ref` and `batch_id` parameters for batch mode (Issue #540). When `feature_ref` is provided, the session record uses schema_version "2.0" and appends per-feature pipeline data to a `features` list, enabling per-feature post-analysis. Single-feature mode writes schema_version "1.0" (backward compatible). Idempotent: duplicate `feature_ref` entries are skipped.
- `unified_session_tracker.py` JSONL activity log entries now include a `feature_ref` field when the `PIPELINE_FEATURE_REF` environment variable is set (Issue #540). This tags each SubagentStop event with the batch feature being processed, enabling per-feature filtering of the activity log.

- `/implement` now writes `explicitly_invoked: true` flag to the pipeline state file (`/tmp/implement_pipeline_state.json`) on startup, enabling `unified_pre_tool.py` to hard-block coordinator code writes during active sessions regardless of `ENFORCEMENT_LEVEL` (Issue #528).

### Changed
- `unified_pre_tool.py` Agent Auth layer (Layer 2) now detects `explicitly_invoked` flag and denies Write/Edit/Bash to code files when the coordinator attempts to bypass pipeline agents during an active `/implement` session. Config files and docs remain exempt.
- Pipeline authorized agents (`PIPELINE_AGENTS`) updated to `implementer`, `test-master`, `doc-master` — removed legacy entries `brownfield-analyzer`, `setup-wizard`, `project-bootstrapper` that no longer exist.

### Fixed
- Terminal-state guard added to `fail_agent()` and `complete_agent()` in `state.py` (Issue #541): both methods are now no-ops when the agent is already in "completed" or "failed" state, preventing a race condition where a late `fail_agent()` call from `unified_session_tracker.py`'s text-scan fallback could overwrite a correctly completed agent.
- `unified_session_tracker.py` status determination now gives priority to `CLAUDE_AGENT_STATUS` environment variable over the `_determine_success()` text scan (Issue #541). The text scan only runs when the env var is absent, eliminating false-failure classifications caused by benign failure-pattern words (e.g. "error handling") in agent output when an authoritative status signal is already present.
- Added `tests/unit/lib/test_agent_tracker_state_idempotency.py` covering terminal-state guard behavior for both `complete_agent()` and `fail_agent()`.
- `unified_session_tracker.py` no longer skips JSONL activity logging when `stop_hook_active=true` is present in the SubagentStop payload (Issue #539). SubagentStop events in consumer repos always carry `stop_hook_active=true`, so the previous early-return guard caused zero activity log entries to be written. The guard has been removed; the hook now logs unconditionally. `_determine_success()` now uses contextual pattern matching to distinguish real failures from benign error mentions (e.g. "error handling", "no errors").
- `RefactorAnalyzer` no longer scans `.worktrees/` or `.claude/` directories, which caused O(n²) timeout on large repos with multiple worktrees (Issue #514). Added `DEFAULT_EXCLUDE_DIRS` class constant (covers version control, caches, worktrees, session logs, archives) and `_should_skip_path()` helper used by all `rglob` traversals. The `__init__` signature now accepts an optional `exclude_dirs` parameter to override the defaults.

## [3.46.0] - 2026-01-09
### Fixed
